# Routes (AppGen)

Use this guide when creating or editing route pages in `src/routes/`.

## Route conventions

- Create routes with `createFileRoute` and keep files focused on page composition.
- Do not edit `src/routeTree.gen.ts` manually (auto-generated).
- **The home route `_protected/index.tsx` is the app's main page.** It ships as a "Welcome…" placeholder and is what the preview opens to (`/`). Build the primary/first page by replacing this file directly — never leave the placeholder, and never put the main page on a separate route while `/` still shows "Welcome…". Rename the matching `Dashboard` entry in `src/components/layout/index.ts` instead of adding a duplicate `/` entry.
- For sidebar pages, add/update the route entry in `src/components/layout/index.ts` so nav and breadcrumbs stay in sync.
- Keep user-facing copy plain and short. AppGen users are non-technical.

## Maintainability rules

- Do not hardcode tenant-specific values (repo names, IDs, account names, URLs) directly in UI text or API calls. If the user provides a URL or repo name as part of their request, put it in a typed constant at the top of the server router file (or better, a `z.url()` procedure input) — never paste it directly into JSX text or hardcode it in two places. UI display labels should derive from the same source, not be re-typed.
- For tRPC procedure calls in routes: follow the `useTRPC` + `queryOptions`/`mutationOptions` pattern. See [`/base/src/integrations/trpc/AGENTS.md`](/base/src/integrations/trpc/AGENTS.md) for the required usage (base-owned; read on demand).
- Prefer existing hooks/services first (the platform hooks in `src/hooks/` and shared services under `@/services/*` already cover auth, connectors, workflows, chat); avoid adding duplicate fetch logic in route files.
- If a page repeats card/row/section markup, use a typed config array or small reusable component.
- Every data page must explicitly handle loading, empty, success, and error states.
- Keep route files readable; split large pages into local view components/hooks when needed.

## Feature handoff notes (required)

When you add a new feature route, append a short subsection here with:

1. Goal and user-visible behavior.
2. Data flow (UI -> hook/service -> API/DB).
3. Inputs/configuration (route params, constants, env, IDs).
4. Constraints or known limitations.
5. Validation commands run (`bun run check`, `bun run build`, `bun run test`).

**Definition of done:** The feature is not complete until the Feature notes subsection below is updated with actual content (not the placeholder line). Writing "Let me update the AGENTS" without then editing this file does not count.

## Feature notes

### AI Insights and Document Pipeline: handling Unstructured Data Connectors

1. **Goal / behavior**: Two related changes for connectors flagged `data_type === "unstructured"` (currently just "One Drive - Unstructured", connector id 1977, whose `unstructured` stream returns whole documents with a `text` field rather than tabular rows). (a) AI Insights' Data Source picker no longer excludes these connectors (a prior temporary restriction, documented in an earlier entry below) — they're selectable again, with their existing reduced row limit (20 vs 200) and per-field text truncation (4,000 chars) kept as-is, plus a clearer error message when the connector's underlying source files are unreachable instead of a raw API error. (b) The Document Intelligence Pipeline page (`/document-pipeline`) gained an "Import from OneDrive (Unstructured)" button that lists documents from the connector directly and starts a pipeline job using the document's `text` field, without requiring a manual file upload first.
2. **Data flow**: No new tables. `document_jobs` gained two nullable columns — `source_text`, `source_connector_name` — via migration `0019_melted_darkhawk.sql`; `storageFileId` is now optional on `CreateDocumentJobInput` (a job is sourced from either an uploaded S3 file OR connector text, never both). `documentPipelineService.ts`'s `runPipelineStage`'s `extract` branch now branches on `job.sourceText`: if present, it's used directly as the raw text handed to the AI "extract and clean" prompt; otherwise the existing S3-download-and-parse path runs unchanged. `document-pipeline.tsx` — new `openConnectorDialog()` calls `useQuerySource()` against connector id `1977` (`SELECT * FROM unstructured`) and renders results in a new Dialog; picking one calls `createDocumentJob` with `sourceText`/`sourceConnectorName` set (no `storageFileId`) and then reuses the existing `runStagesSequentially` orchestrator unchanged. `insights.tsx` — removed the `.filter((c) => c.attributes.configuration?.data_type !== "unstructured")` step from the `sourceConnectors` memo; added a `friendlyConnectorError()` helper (both pages) that rewrites `itemNotFound`/"resource could not be found" errors into a plain-language explanation that the connector's source files are unreachable, rather than surfacing the raw API error.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Connector id `1977` ("One Drive - Unstructured") is a hardcoded module-level constant in `document-pipeline.tsx`, confirmed with the user before implementing.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Live-verified via the `aisquared` MCP tools that connector 1977's `unstructured` stream currently fails with a real `itemNotFound` error from OneDrive itself (the configured file(s) appear to have been moved/deleted) — this is a source-connection problem outside the app's control; the `friendlyConnectorError()` messaging surfaces this clearly instead of a raw error, but doesn't fix the underlying connection (that needs the connector repointed by whoever manages the OneDrive account). Document Pipeline's connector import only supports this one connector/stream today (no generic "pick any unstructured connector" picker) — extending it to other unstructured sources would need the same pattern repeated with a different connector id/table name.
5. **Validation**: Live-verified connector 1977's schema (`unstructured` stream: `text`, `filename`, `filetype`, `element_id`, `created_date`, `modified_date`) via `aisquared_connectors_discover`, and reproduced the real `itemNotFound` query failure via `aisquared_connectors_query_source` before writing the error-messaging code. `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `document_jobs` gained the two new columns, table list otherwise unchanged). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors across all touched files (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched files; `bunx biome check` showed only the same pre-existing `useExhaustiveDependencies` patterns already present in `insights.tsx` (deliberate, documented in earlier entries above) — not a new regression. Confirmed `/insights` and `/document-pipeline` still return 200 from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v2.51.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done.

### Fix: OneDrive Unstructured connector errors showed a bare "422" instead of an explanation

1. **Goal / behavior**: The "Import from OneDrive (Unstructured)" dialog (Document Pipeline) and AI Insights' data loading for unstructured connectors now always show a clear, plain-language explanation when the connector can't be reached, instead of a raw `AIS API request failed with status 422` (or similar bare-status) message.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/data change. Root cause: the base platform request helper (`/base/src/server/platform/request.ts`'s `handleErrorResponse`) only extracts a detail message when the error response body has a top-level `message` field — this connector's real error body doesn't match that shape, so the app's generic error path falls back to a bare `AIS API request failed with status <code>` string with no way to regex-match the real underlying reason (confirmed live: the true cause is OneDrive returning `itemNotFound`, but that text never reaches the app). `document-pipeline.tsx`'s `friendlyConnectorError()` was simplified to always return the same clear explanation for any error from the OneDrive Unstructured import dialog (it's a single fixed connector with a known failure mode, so there was no need to pattern-match the message at all). `insights.tsx`'s `friendlyConnectorError()` now also treats any `AIS API request failed` message, or any error at all when the currently selected connector `isUnstructuredConnector`, as this same category — not just messages literally containing `itemNotFound`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: `insights.tsx`'s broadened check means a *different*, unrelated failure on an unstructured connector (not a connection issue) would still show this same "can't be reached" message rather than its own specific cause — an acceptable tradeoff given unstructured connectors in this app are current a single connector with only this one observed failure mode.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors in either touched file (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on both touched files. Confirmed `/insights` and `/document-pipeline` still return 200 from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v2.51.1) and a To-Do item documenting the fix, marked done.

### Removed the Unstructured connector option (again)

1. **Goal / behavior**: Per a follow-up request, unstructured document connector support was removed entirely — the underlying OneDrive connector kept failing (422/`itemNotFound`) regardless of the clearer error messaging added in the previous fix, so the option itself was pulled rather than continuing to surface an unreliable connector. AI Insights' Data Source picker no longer lists unstructured connectors (back to the same filter documented in an earlier entry above); the Document Pipeline page's "Import from OneDrive (Unstructured)" button and dialog are gone entirely.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/migration change. `insights.tsx` — restored the `sourceConnectors` filter excluding `configuration.data_type === "unstructured"` connectors (the `isUnstructuredConnector`/`TABLE_ROW_LIMIT`/truncation logic stays in place but dormant, same as before it was briefly re-enabled). `document-pipeline.tsx` — removed the `useQuerySource` import, the `ONEDRIVE_UNSTRUCTURED_CONNECTOR_ID`/`_NAME` constants, the `UnstructuredDoc` type, all related state (`connectorDialogOpen`/`connectorDocs`/`connectorError`/`importingDocId`), the `friendlyConnectorError`/`openConnectorDialog`/`handleImportConnectorDoc` functions, the "Import from OneDrive (Unstructured)" button, and its Dialog markup. `document_jobs.source_text`/`source_connector_name` (added for this feature) and `documentPipelineService.ts`'s connector-sourced-job support in `createDocumentJob`/`runPipelineStage` were left in place unused, rather than reverted with another migration — dormant, matching this app's convention for temporarily-disabled features elsewhere.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: If unstructured-connector support is wanted again later, the dormant pieces (schema columns, service support, `isUnstructuredConnector` logic in `insights.tsx`) can be reactivated without a new migration — only the UI filter/removed dialog would need to come back.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors across both touched files (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` on both touched files — no new lint issues. Confirmed `/insights` and `/document-pipeline` still return 200 from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v2.52.0) and a To-Do item documenting the removal, marked done.

### New page: Export Center (`/export-center`)

1. **Goal / behavior**: A new page (Files & Media nav group) for exporting any of 15 curated data tables to CSV, optionally narrowed to a From/To date range on the table's creation date. Every export is uploaded to Storage and tracked in an "Export history" list on the same page, with per-row Download (presigned URL, opens in a new tab) and Delete (removes both the Storage file and its history row) actions. Distinct from the existing full-database JSON snapshot/restore feature already on `/settings` → Backups — this is a selective, single-table, spreadsheet-friendly export tool, not a whole-database backup.
2. **Data flow**: New `data_exports` table in `src/server/db/schema.ts` (`tableKey`, `tableLabel`, `storageFileId` FK cascade to `storageFiles`, `rowCount`, nullable `fromDate`/`toDate`), migrated via `db:generate`/`db:migrate` (migration `0020_fuzzy_triathlon.sql`). New `src/services/db/exportCenterService.ts`: `EXPORT_TABLES` is a hardcoded allow-list of 15 tables (`todos`, `errorLogs`, `auditLogs`, `changelogEntries`, `inventoryItems`, `chatConversations`, `contentCalendarItems`, `workflowRuns`, `savedQueries`, `aiInsights`, `webhooks`, `integrations`, `workspaceModels`, `csvImportBatches`, `documentJobs`) — every one confirmed to have a `createdAt` column, used both for the optional date-range filter and to order rows newest-first. `runExport({table, from, to})` queries the table (dynamic lookup by name against the `schema` module, same pattern already used by `backupService.ts`/`exportService.ts`), converts to CSV via the existing shared `rowsToCsv` (`@/lib/csvExport`), uploads to `exports/center/<table>-<timestamp>.csv` via the existing `uploadFile`, inserts a `storageFiles` row, then a `dataExports` row linking to it. `listExportHistory()` joins `dataExports` + `storageFiles` for the history table. `deleteExport`/`getExportDownloadUrl` mirror the existing `deleteFile`/`getPresignedUrl` patterns used elsewhere (`exportService.ts`, `reportService.ts`). `export-center.tsx` is a standard `useQuery`/`useMutation` page gated on `useCapabilities()` (`databaseEnabled && s3Enabled`), logging each export via `useActivityLog().logAudit` (category `export`).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses `DATABASE_URL` and the already-provisioned S3 storage. Date-range inputs are plain `<Input type="date">` fields (no calendar-range picker component exists in this app yet) — consistent with the To-Do page's own `dueDate` field style.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: `apiKeys` (holds a real secret in `keyValue`) is deliberately excluded from `EXPORT_TABLES` to avoid ever writing a credential into a downloadable CSV file — this was a conscious design choice, not an oversight, given the user explicitly wanted this page to touch Database/Secrets/Storage together; a handful of other internal/detail tables (theme snapshots, pipeline stage detail rows, CSV import row staging, gallery image metadata) are also excluded as not useful standalone exports. Per an explicit user request, this page does **not** generate expiring/password-protected "signed" share links — downloads use the same plain presigned-URL pattern (1-hour expiry, authenticated users only) already used throughout the app, not a separate public-sharing feature. Exports are point-in-time snapshots generated on click, not scheduled/recurring.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `data_exports` in the table list). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors across all created/touched files (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` on all created/touched files — no new lint issues. Confirmed `/export-center` returns 200 from the dev server and `routeTree.gen.ts` picked up the new route. Added a Changelog entry (v2.53.0) and a To-Do item documenting the new page, marked done.

### Fix: Storage file preview text was hard to read

1. **Goal / behavior**: The `FilePreviewDialog` (used by `/storage`) text-file preview is now easier to read: a wider dialog for text files, a bigger monospace font with more line height, real syntax highlighting for JSON/XML/HTML/Markdown, and a visible notice when a large file's preview was truncated (previously the `truncated` flag from `getStorageFileText` was fetched but silently discarded).
2. **Data flow**: No schema/service change — `getStorageFileText`'s existing `{ text, truncated }` response is unchanged; only the dialog component's rendering changed. `FilePreviewDialog.tsx` — new `EXTENSION_LANGUAGE`/`languageForFileName()` maps a file's extension to a Shiki grammar name (`json`/`xml`/`html`/`markdown`, else plain `text`); the plain `<pre className="text-xs">` block was replaced with the shared `CodeBlockCode` component (`@/components/ui/code-block`, the same Shiki-backed renderer already used by `SqlEditor.tsx`), themed `css-variables` (same theme choice as `SqlEditor.tsx`, for light/dark consistency) with a larger `text-sm leading-relaxed` override. New `textTruncated` state (from the previously-ignored `truncated` field) drives an `Alert` shown above the preview when true. The dialog's `DialogContent` width is now `sm:max-w-4xl` specifically for text-preview files (image/PDF/video/audio previews keep the original `sm:max-w-3xl`).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: CSV/log/plain-text files still render as plain monospace (no highlighting) since Shiki has no meaningful grammar for them — only the structured formats (JSON/XML/HTML/Markdown) get real syntax coloring. This only touches the file-preview dialog; other "preview" surfaces in the app (Import Wizard's CSV preview table, Theme Generator's live preview, Knowledge Base's result snippets, Gallery's image preview) were confirmed with the user to be out of scope and left unchanged.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` on the touched file — the one lint finding (`useAnchorContent` on the existing Download `<a>` button) predates this change (confirmed via `grep`, that line was untouched). Confirmed `/storage` still returns 200 from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v2.54.0) and a To-Do item documenting the fix, marked done.

### Fix: CSV preview rendered as plain text, not columns

1. **Goal / behavior**: Correction to the entry above — the user's actual complaint was specifically that `.csv` files in the Storage preview dialog rendered as one flat line of comma-separated text rather than columns/rows. CSV files now render as a real table (header row + data rows), while every other text file type keeps the syntax-highlighted code view from the previous fix.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/service change. `FilePreviewDialog.tsx` — new `isCsvFile()` check (content type `text/csv` or a `.csv` extension) and a `csvRows`/`csvColumns` derivation using the existing pure `parseCsv` helper (`@/lib/csvImport`, already used by Import Wizard) against the already-fetched preview text. A new render branch (checked before the generic `CodeBlockCode` branch) renders `csvRows`/`csvColumns` through the shared `Table`/`TableHeader`/`TableBody` components, capped at a new `CSV_PREVIEW_ROW_LIMIT` (200) with a note when more rows exist in the fetched (possibly server-truncated) chunk.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: This is a second, client-side cap on top of the existing server-side text-truncation in `getStorageFileText` — a CSV whose first N characters (the server's truncation point) cut off mid-row will show that partial last row's cells shifted/incomplete, same edge case the plain-text preview already had, just now visible as a table row instead of a text fragment.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` on the touched file — same single pre-existing `useAnchorContent` finding as before, unrelated to this change. Confirmed `/storage` still returns 200 from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v2.54.1) and a To-Do item documenting the fix, marked done.

### Improvement: wrap-text toggle for CSV preview

1. **Goal / behavior**: The CSV table preview (see entry above) gained a "Wrap text" toggle button, shown above the table whenever a CSV is being previewed. Off (default) keeps every cell on one line with horizontal scrolling, matching the original behavior; on, cell content wraps onto multiple lines so long values are fully visible without scrolling sideways.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/service change, purely a client-side rendering toggle. `FilePreviewDialog.tsx` — new `wrapCsvText` boolean state; `TableHead`/`TableCell` className switches between `whitespace-nowrap` and `whitespace-normal break-words align-top` based on it.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: The toggle only affects the CSV table view; it has no effect on the syntax-highlighted code view used for other text file types (those already wrap via the existing `[&>pre]:whitespace-pre-wrap` class).
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` — same single pre-existing `useAnchorContent` finding, unrelated. Confirmed `/storage` still returns 200 from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v2.54.2) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done.

### Full app rebuild: Dashboard + 13 pages (multi-page layout)

1. **Goal / behavior**: Rebuilt the app as a multi-page dashboard: Dashboard (home `/`), AI Document Summariser, Audit Log, Changelog, AI Chat, Document Archive, Error Log, Image Gallery, Performance Monitor, Settings, Storage, Theme Generator, To-Do, Weather Dashboard. Switched `_protected.tsx` from `AppShell` to `AppLayout` (sidebar nav) and updated `src/components/layout/index.ts` with all nav entries.
2. **Data flow**: New Drizzle tables in `src/server/db/schema.ts` (`todos`, `errorLogs`, `auditLogs`, `changelogEntries`, `chatConversations`, `chatMessages`, `storageFiles`), migrated via `db:generate`/`db:migrate`. New domain services in `src/services/db/{todoService,auditLogService,errorLogService,changelogService,chatService,storageService}.ts` using `createServerFn` + `requireAppAccess()`. Routes call these via TanStack Query (`useQuery`/`useMutation`), gated by `useCapabilities()` (`databaseEnabled`, `s3Enabled`). AI features (`chat.tsx`, `ai-summariser.tsx`) use `useExecuteModel()` against Anthropic (1319), OpenAI (1751), AI Squared Bolt 2 (1803) source connectors with the `{ messages }` payload path.
3. **Inputs/config**: DB via `DATABASE_URL` (Neon, provisioned). S3 via platform-provisioned storage credentials (`storageService.ts`, folders `uploads`/`exports`/`assets`). Weather page calls public Open-Meteo APIs directly from the client (no key). Settings and Theme Generator persist to `localStorage` only. To-Do falls back to localStorage (seeded with 3 example tasks) when the database is off.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Documents page excludes image files (shown in Gallery instead); Gallery/Storage/Documents require both DB and S3 — show an alert/empty state otherwise. Base UI (not Radix) primitives are used throughout: `Button`/`DialogTrigger`/`AlertDialogTrigger`/`PopoverTrigger` use the `render={<Element/>}` prop instead of `asChild`; `Select.onValueChange` and `ToggleGroup.onValueChange` receive `string | null` / `string[]`, not the raw new value directly — always null/array-guard before calling state setters.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (7 tables confirmed connected). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` run after building all pages — fixed all app-level type errors (Base UI `render` prop, nullable `onValueChange` values, unused variable, array-typed slider/toggle values). `bunx biome format --write` run on every touched file. Did not run `bun run build` or full `bun run check` per project rules (dev-server + LSP/tsc used for verification instead).

### Fix: Theme Generator not applying, Chat page jank

1. **Goal / behavior**: (a) Activating a saved theme on `/theme` now visibly applies app-wide (or page-wide for page-scoped themes), not just inside that page's own preview card. (b) Typing in the AI Chat composer no longer re-renders/re-parses markdown for every already-sent message on each keystroke.
2. **Data flow**: Extracted the theme read/write helpers from `theme.tsx` into shared `src/lib/customTheme.ts` (`readThemes`/`writeThemes`/`readActiveMap`/`writeActiveMap`/`resolveActiveTheme`), which now also dispatch a `custom-theme-updated` window event on every write. New `src/components/theme/ThemeApplier.tsx` is mounted once in `_protected.tsx` (above `AppLayout`, so it runs on every route): it listens for that event plus the cross-tab `storage` event and the current pathname (`useRouterState`), resolves the active theme (page-specific overrides global), and renders a fixed full-viewport background + blur/overlay layer at `z-0`, toggling a `custom-theme-active` class on `<html>`. `src/styles.css` adds rules scoped to that class that make the sidebar/header/cards translucent with `backdrop-filter: blur(...)` (via `data-slot` attributes already present on the shared `Sidebar`/`Card` components) so the theme layer shows through. `chat.tsx` now renders messages through a module-level `memo(MessageBubble)` wrapper so React skips re-rendering (and re-parsing markdown for) messages whose data hasn't changed when the composer's `input` state updates the page.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Reuses existing `localStorage` keys `custom-themes-v1` / `active-theme-v1`.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Only the app chrome (sidebar, header, cards) gets the translucent glass treatment; deeply nested custom-styled elements without `data-slot="card"` won't automatically pick up the effect. `ThemeApplier`'s background layer is `pointer-events-none` and non-interactive by design.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched files. Verified via dev server that `/`, `/theme`, `/chat` still respond (307 auth redirect, no 500s). Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Persist saved themes and active theme to the database

1. **Goal / behavior**: The Theme Generator's saved themes and the "active theme" selection (per page or global) now persist in the database instead of the browser's `localStorage`, so an applied theme survives reloads, new browser sessions, and shows the same on any device/session.
2. **Data flow**: Added `custom_themes` and `active_themes` tables to `src/server/db/schema.ts` (`active_themes.scope_key` -> FK to `custom_themes.id`, cascade delete). New `src/services/db/themeService.ts` (`listCustomThemes`, `createCustomTheme`, `deleteCustomTheme`, `listActiveThemes`, `setActiveTheme`, `clearActiveTheme`, `clearActiveThemes`) using `createServerFn` + `requireAppAccess()`. `src/lib/customTheme.ts` was trimmed to shared types/constants/presets/`resolveActiveTheme` (now takes the active map as a parameter instead of reading `localStorage`). `src/routes/_protected/theme.tsx` and `src/components/theme/ThemeApplier.tsx` now use `useQuery`/`useMutation` against `themeService` instead of the old `readThemes`/`writeThemes`/`readActiveMap`/`writeActiveMap` localStorage helpers, gated by `useCapabilities().databaseEnabled`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses the existing `DATABASE_URL`. Theme `id` changed from a client-generated `crypto.randomUUID()` string to a DB-generated integer identity column.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: When the database isn't configured, the Theme page shows a banner and the Save button is disabled — the previous localStorage fallback was removed, so themes are database-only. One-time migration hiccup during rollout: the `drizzle.__drizzle_migrations` tracking table was empty even though earlier tables already existed in the DB, causing `db:migrate` to try re-creating them; fixed by inserting matching hash/timestamp rows for the already-applied migrations before re-running `db:migrate` for the new tables only — no data was dropped or altered.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + manual migration-tracking backfill + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `custom_themes`/`active_themes` in the table list). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched files. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Settings/Configuration Panel: API Keys, Webhooks, Integrations

1. **Goal / behavior**: Extended the existing `/settings` page (rather than adding a new route) with three new tabs — **API Keys**, **Webhooks**, **Integrations** — so users can manage secrets, outbound webhook subscriptions, and third-party integration toggles from one place, completing the "Build Settings/Configuration Panel page" To-Do item.
2. **Data flow**: New `api_keys`, `webhooks`, `integrations` tables in `src/server/db/schema.ts`, migrated via `db:generate`/`db:migrate`. New `src/services/db/configPanelService.ts` (`createServerFn` + `requireAppAccess()`) with list/create/delete + status-toggle procedures for each; every mutation also inserts a row into the existing `audit_logs` table (category `"settings"`) so changes show up on the Audit Log page. Three new presentational components — `src/components/settings/ApiKeysPanel.tsx`, `WebhooksPanel.tsx`, `IntegrationsPanel.tsx` — each fetch/mutate via TanStack Query and are rendered inside new `TabsContent` panels in `src/routes/_protected/settings.tsx`, gated on `useCapabilities().databaseEnabled`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses `DATABASE_URL`. API key values are stored as plain text in the database (this is a demo app, not real secret storage) but are always masked in the UI by default (`•••1234`) with an explicit reveal/copy action per row — never logged or printed. Webhook `event` is one of a fixed enum (`record.created/updated/deleted`, `user.signed_in`, `error.raised`); no real HTTP calls are actually dispatched to the configured URLs (there's no event bus in this template to trigger them from) — this panel manages the *configuration*, matching the To-Do's "secure management of API keys, webhooks, and integrations" scope.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: No true encryption-at-rest for key values (Postgres column, no envelope encryption) — acceptable for this demo/config-management scope, called out to the user. Webhooks are not actually triggered by app events. Panels are database-only (no localStorage fallback) — show an inline message when `databaseEnabled` is false.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `api_keys`/`webhooks`/`integrations` in the table list). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched files. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Workflows page (`/workflows`) and Connectors page (`/connectors`)

1. **Goal / behavior**: `/workflows` lists every workflow connected to the app with status/trigger badges + summary counts, runs any of them from a dialog (input message -> result shown inline), and keeps a Run History tab (input, result, status, duration). Workflows can be added by ID or removed. `/connectors` lists all workspace connectors live with search + source/destination filter and summary counts; selecting one opens a detail sheet with type/provider/enabled/configuration and, for SQL sources, a **Discover Schema** action listing real tables/columns; a **Request Connector** form records requests for an admin. Both registered in a new "Platform" nav group in `src/components/layout/index.ts`.
2. **Data flow**: **Workflows** — new `workflow_registry` + `workflow_runs` tables and `src/services/db/workflowRegistryService.ts` (list/register/unregister, list/record/clear runs; all mutations write `audit_logs` with category `workflows`). `src/server/db/seed-workflows.ts` seeds the 16 workspace workflows (idempotent via `onConflictDoNothing` on `workflow_id`). `src/components/workflows/WorkflowRunDialog.tsx` calls `useRunWorkflow(workflowId)` (from `@/hooks/useWorkflows`) plus `useWorkflowDataAppConfig` (from `@/hooks/useDataAppSessions`) to forward `dataAppId`/`dataAppToken` when the workflow has them, and parses the response with `extractAssistantContent` from `@/hooks/useChatAssistant`. It is rendered *per active run* (keyed by workflow id) because `useRunWorkflow` binds to a single workflow id and cannot be called per-row. **Connectors** — `useConnectors({ per_page: 100 })` / `useConnector(id)` / `useQuerySource()` from `@/hooks/useConnectors` (base tRPC); new `connector_requests` table + `src/services/db/connectorRequestService.ts` (audit-logged, category `connectors`).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env. Workflow run payload shape is `{ workflow: { inputs: { message } } }`. Schema discovery runs a fixed `information_schema.columns` query through `querySource` and is gated to a `SQL_PROVIDERS` allow-list (Postgresql, MicrosoftSql, MariaDB, Oracle, Snowflake, Clickhouse, Databricks, Bigquery, AwsAthena); the connector's `configuration.credentials` is explicitly filtered out before rendering.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: **The runtime API has no workflow-list procedure** — hence the DB-backed `workflow_registry` seeded from the platform's list (it will not auto-sync if workflows are added/renamed in the platform; re-run the seed or add by ID). **There is also no create-connector, update, delete, discover/catalog, or connector-definitions procedure in the app's tRPC layer** (those exist only as `aisquared` MCP tools), so "create" is implemented as a recorded *request* rather than live creation, and discovery is SQL-introspection rather than the platform's own catalog API. `information_schema` filtering uses Postgres-style `table_schema NOT IN ('pg_catalog','information_schema')`, so results may vary for non-Postgres engines.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `workflow_registry`, `workflow_runs`, `connector_requests`). Seed run: registry reports 16 entries. `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). Confirmed `routeTree.gen.ts` auto-picked up both new routes and that `/workflows` + `/connectors` return 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. `bunx biome format --write` on all touched files. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Fix: Workflow run 400 + Connector schema discovery 422

1. **Goal / behavior**: (a) Running a workflow from `/workflows` no longer fails with a 400; the Run button is now disabled with an explanation for workflows that aren't published with a chat interface (they always fail server-side regardless of input key). (b) "Discover Schema" on `/connectors` no longer 422s for engines whose catalog isn't `information_schema`-compatible.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. `src/components/workflows/WorkflowRunDialog.tsx` — fixed the run payload from `{ inputs: { message } }` to `{ inputs: { text } }` (verified against live workflow runs: the platform's `chat_input` trigger component's own field is `text`), added a per-`dataAppId` session id via `getOrCreateSessionId` (`@/lib/sessionManager`, same as the working `useChatAssistant`), and gated the whole run UI behind `canRun = !!dataAppId && !!dataAppToken` from `useWorkflowDataAppConfig` — draft/unconfigured workflows show an `Alert` instead of attempting a run. `src/components/connectors/ConnectorDetailSheet.tsx` — narrowed `SQL_PROVIDERS` (the discovery allow-list) from 9 engines down to `Postgresql` and `MariaDB` only.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Verified live via the `aisquared` MCP tools before narrowing: `Postgresql`/`MariaDB` return real schema rows; `Oracle` throws `ORA-00942: table or view "INFORMATION_SCHEMA"."COLUMNS" does not exist`; `Snowflake`'s test connector has an unrelated broken ODBC driver. Workflow input: sending `{ text }` to a **published** workflow with a configured data-app succeeds; unpublished/unconfigured workflows fail with `Missing input 'user_prompt'` regardless of the key sent (that field is an internal edge-wiring name between components, not something an external caller sets) — so those are now excluded from running rather than key-guessed.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Discovery still won't work for Oracle/Snowflake/ClickHouse/Databricks/BigQuery/Athena sources — their configuration/details still display, just without live schema browsing. Workflows without a published chat trigger can't be run from this app at all (by design, matching how the platform itself gates it) — only "Add Workflow"/"Remove" and browsing remain available for those.
5. **Validation**: Verified request/response shapes directly against live connectors and workflows via the `aisquared` MCP tools (not just static analysis) before changing code. `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on both touched files. Confirmed `/workflows` and `/connectors` still return 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Fix: Workflow run still 400'ing on published-but-unconfigured workflows + missing Error Log entries

1. **Goal / behavior**: (a) `WorkflowRunDialog` was still letting users attempt to run workflows that have a chat interface configured but aren't `published` yet, which the platform rejects with a bare 400 (no body message) — Run is now also gated on `status === "published"`, with a message that distinguishes "no chat interface" from "not published". (b) Failures on `/workflows` and `/connectors` (run failures, schema-discovery failures, connector/connector-list load failures) weren't reaching the Error Log — they now call `createErrorLog` the same way `chat.tsx`/`ai-summariser.tsx` already do.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. `WorkflowRunDialog.tsx` — `canRun` now also checks `config?.data?.attributes.status === "published"`; its `onError` calls `createErrorLog` (gated on `useCapabilities().databaseEnabled`, source `"Workflows"`). `ConnectorDetailSheet.tsx` — added `error` from `useConnector` surfaced as a destructive `Alert` (previously silently showed nothing but skeletons forever) plus a `useEffect` that logs it once, and the schema-discovery `onError` now also logs. `connectors.tsx` — added a `useEffect` on the `useConnectors` list error to log it too.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Verified live via the `aisquared` MCP tools: of the 16 seeded workflows, only 3 have a `configuration.interface.dataAppId`/`dataAppToken` pair at all, and one of those three ("Chatbot") is still `draft`.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: The platform gives no error detail for the "not published" case (bare 400), so the UI's explanation is inferred from workflow state (interface presence + status) rather than parsed from the server response. Error Log entries from these pages are fire-and-forget (`.catch(() => {})`) and skipped entirely when the database isn't configured, matching the existing convention elsewhere in the app.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all three touched files. Confirmed `/workflows`, `/connectors`, `/error-log` still return 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Correction: Run guard was over-restrictive — dataAppId/dataAppToken are optional, not required

1. **Goal / behavior**: The previous fix required a `dataAppId`/`dataAppToken` pair to be present on top of `status === "published"`, which blocked Run on **published** workflows that simply don't have a chat-widget data-app assigned (most of them) — the exact bug report was "shows the disabled message even for a published workflow."
2. **Data flow**: `WorkflowRunDialog.tsx` — `canRun` is now just `!configLoading && status === "published"`. `dataAppId`/`dataAppToken`/`dataAppSessionId` are still sent to `useRunWorkflow` when present (for chat session continuity on the 3 workflows that do have a data-app configured), but are no longer required — verified live via the `aisquared` MCP `workflows_run` tool that a published workflow runs successfully with just `{ inputs: { text } }` and no data-app headers at all.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Draft workflows still can't be run (by design — the platform itself rejects those). If a workflow's `getWorkflowDataAppConfig` call is slow/fails, `configLoading`/`isPublished` briefly show the draft-only guard — no separate loading-vs-draft distinction is made beyond the existing `Skeleton`.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on the touched file. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Connectors page: AI/ML vs Data category filter for sources

1. **Goal / behavior**: When "Sources only" is selected in the Type filter on `/connectors`, a second **Category** select appears (All categories / AI/ML / Data) to narrow the source list further. Selecting a different Type resets the category back to "all" since the split only applies to sources.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. `connectors.tsx` now makes two `useConnectors` calls: an unfiltered one (`allConnectorsRes`, `per_page: 100`) used solely to compute the summary cards (Total/Sources/Destinations) so they always reflect the whole workspace, and a filtered one that forwards `type` and (when `typeFilter === "source"`) `category` straight to the platform's connectors API — verified live via the `aisquared` MCP tools that `category: "ai_ml"` and `category: "data"` both return the expected connector subsets server-side. The client-side `filtered` memo now only applies the free-text search on top of that server-filtered result (type/category filtering was moved server-side, no longer duplicated client-side).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. `ListConnectorsParams.category` is untyped (`string`) in the base types, so the two literal values used here (`"ai_ml"`, `"data"`) are an app-level convention matched to what the platform actually recognizes — not enforced by the type system.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: The category split is only offered for sources (destinations aren't meaningfully split this way in the platform's own connector catalog). If the platform introduces new categories, the two hardcoded `SelectItem`s would need updating manually — there's no categories-list endpoint to read them from dynamically.
5. **Validation**: Verified `category=ai_ml` and `category=data` filtering live against the workspace's real connectors via `aisquared_connectors_list` before wiring the UI. `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on the touched file. Confirmed `/connectors` still returns 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Query page (`/query`)

1. **Goal / behavior**: New page with three tabs — **Run Query** (pick any source connector, write/run SQL via `useQuerySource`, view results in a table), **Saved Queries** (save the current connector+SQL by name, load it back into the editor, or delete it), and **Models** (read-only cards listing the workspace's defined Models — name, query type badge, underlying query, connector). Registered in the "Platform" nav group alongside Workflows/Connectors.
2. **Data flow**: New `saved_queries` table + `src/services/db/savedQueryService.ts` (list/create/delete, audit-logged category `query`). New `workspace_models` table + `src/services/db/workspaceModelService.ts` (list only — read-only). `src/server/db/seed-models.ts` seeds a snapshot of 17 models captured from the platform (idempotent via `onConflictDoNothing` on `model_id`). Query execution reuses `useConnectors({ type: "source" })` for the connector picker and `useQuerySource()` for running arbitrary SQL — same hooks as the Connectors page's schema discovery, no new base/tRPC code.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Query failures call `createErrorLog` (gated on `databaseEnabled`, source `"Query"`), matching the pattern used on `/workflows` and `/connectors`.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: **There is no runtime API to list workspace Models** (only the `aisquared` MCP tools can read it, same limitation as the Workflows list) — the Models tab is a static snapshot rather than a live feed; ask the agent to re-run `seed-models.ts` with fresh data if models change in the platform. Query results are shown as opaque strings per cell (no type-specific formatting) and there's no query timeout/row-limit enforced client-side beyond whatever the underlying `querySource` call itself applies.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `saved_queries`, `workspace_models`). Seed run: models registry reports 17 entries. `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). Confirmed `routeTree.gen.ts` picked up the new route and `/query` returns 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. `bunx biome format --write` on all touched files. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Query page: select a model to use as a query, exclude AI/ML and Unstructured models

1. **Goal / behavior**: The Models tab (and a new model picker in the Query Editor card) now filter out models with `queryType === "ai_ml"` or `"unstructured"` since those don't run as SQL. Each remaining model card has a **Use in Query** button, and there's a "Start from a model" dropdown above the SQL editor — both load the model's `connectorId`/`query` into the editor and switch the page to the Run Query tab.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. `query.tsx` — `models` is now a `useMemo` filter over the raw `listWorkspaceModels()` result (`allModels`); `handleUseModel` validates the model has both a `query` and a `connectorId` that matches one of the currently available source connectors (`sourceConnectors`) before loading it, since a model's connector could in theory no longer exist/be a source. `Tabs` is now controlled (`activeTab` state) so selecting a model can programmatically switch to the "run" tab.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: If a model's `connectorId` doesn't match any connector currently returned by `useConnectors({ type: "source" })` (e.g. deleted or now a destination), "Use in Query" shows an error toast instead of loading — this can only be verified against the live connector list at click time, not baked into the static `workspace_models` snapshot.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only; fixed one new type error from an initial `Select value={undefined}` attempt by leaving the picker uncontrolled). `bunx biome format --write` on the touched file. Confirmed `/query` still returns 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Live HR Dashboard, AI Insights page, and New Data Source Wizard

1. **Goal / behavior**: Added `/data-dashboard` — a live dashboard showing real-time employee/department metrics (total employees, active count, department count, average salary, headcount-by-department bar chart, employment-status pie chart, employee table) pulled directly from a connected source connector on every load + manual "Refresh". Added `/insights` — an AI-powered insights page: "Generate Insights" queries the same live data, feeds a compact text summary to an AI/ML model connector, renders the returned markdown insight, and saves each generation to a new history list (deletable). Upgraded the "Request Connector" dialog on `/connectors` into a 4-step `ConnectorWizardDialog` (Category -> Provider -> Details -> Review) — button renamed "New Data Source". Both new pages registered under a new "Live Insights" sidebar group in `src/components/layout/index.ts`.
2. **Data flow**: `/data-dashboard` and `/insights` both call `useQuerySource()` (from `@/hooks/useConnectors`) directly against connector id `2108` ("Supabase Connector App Gen", a Postgresql source with an `employees`/`departments` HR dataset) — no new server procedure, reusing the existing base tRPC `connectors.querySource`. `/insights` also calls `useExecuteModel()` against AI/ML connector id `1803` ("Ai Squared Bolt 2") with a `{ messages, instructions }` payload; the model's OpenAI-style `data[0].choices[0].message.content` response shape is parsed by a local `extractModelText` helper (distinct from `useChatAssistant`'s `extractAssistantContent`, which expects the workflow-run response shape, not the raw `executeModel` shape). New `ai_insights` table + `src/services/db/aiInsightService.ts` (`listAiInsights`/`createAiInsight`/`deleteAiInsight`, audit-logged category `insights`) persists generated insights; gated on `useCapabilities().databaseEnabled`. The wizard (`src/components/connectors/ConnectorWizardDialog.tsx`) still calls the existing `createConnectorRequest` (`connector_requests` table) at the final step — no live connector-creation call, since the app's tRPC layer has no such procedure (documented in the "Workflows page and Connectors page" entry above).
3. **Inputs/config**: Connector ids `2108` (data source) and `1803` (AI model) are hardcoded module-level constants in both new route files — chosen and confirmed with the user via the `aisquared` MCP tools during the build (live schema discovery showed a small 5-employee/4-department dataset with empty `leave_requests`/`leave_balances`/`employee_benefits`, so the dashboard focuses on employee/department metrics only). No new env/config.

### New page: Secrets & API Keys Vault (`/secrets`)

1. **Goal / behavior**: A new page (Platform nav group) for managing API keys/secrets used by the app. Lists all keys in a table (name, masked value with reveal/copy actions, status badge including a computed "expired" state, created/last-used/expires dates) with per-row Rotate/Revoke-Reactivate/Delete actions (delete behind an `AlertDialog` confirmation). A "New Secret" dialog generates a key and shows the full value once in a copy-to-clipboard box — it is never shown in full again after the dialog is closed.
2. **Data flow**: Extended the existing (previously unused by any page) `api_keys` table in `src/server/db/schema.ts` with two new nullable columns, `lastUsedAt` and `expiresAt` (both `timestamp`), migrated via `db:generate`/`db:migrate` (migration `0024_amused_sauron.sql`). New `src/services/db/apiKeyService.ts` (`createServerFn` + `requireAppAccess()`) with `listApiKeys`, `createApiKey` (auto-generates a random `sk_live_<40 hex chars>` secret value), `rotateApiKey` (generates a new secret value for an existing key and clears `lastUsedAt`), `revokeApiKey`, `reactivateApiKey`, `deleteApiKey`, and `touchApiKey` (marks a key's `lastUsedAt` as now — called whenever a key is copied or its value is revealed). `secrets.tsx` is a standard `useQuery`/`useMutation` page gated on `useCapabilities().databaseEnabled` with loading/empty states; every mutation logs via `useActivityLog().logAudit` under category `"secrets"` so changes show up on the Audit Log page.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses `DATABASE_URL`. Registered in `src/components/layout/index.ts` under the existing "Platform" nav group (title "Secrets", url `/secrets`, icon `KeyRound` from `lucide-react`).
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Same as the earlier Settings API Keys panel entry above — key values are stored as plain text in the database (demo app, not real secret storage), always masked in the UI by default with an explicit reveal/copy action, never logged. This page is independent of and does not replace the API Keys tab on `/settings`; the two currently manage separate rows in the same `api_keys` table rather than being merged into one UI.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed the `api_keys` column count increased, no new tables). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` showed no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` errors only). `bunx biome format --write` was run on all touched/created files. Added a Changelog entry (v2.74.0) and a To-Do item documenting the new page, marked done.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: If the underlying dataset grows leave/benefits records, the dashboard and insights prompt don't yet surface them — would need new queries added to both pages. The insights prompt sends the full employee list as plain text (fine at current data volumes; would need summarization/chunking for a much larger dataset). The wizard, like the original request form, only records a request — live connector creation still requires an administrator to complete it in the AI Squared platform.
5. **Validation**: Live-verified connector id 2108's schema and connector id 1803's chat-completion response shape directly via the `aisquared` MCP tools (`connectors_discover`, `connectors_query_source`, `connectors_execute_model`) before wiring up the pages. `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `ai_insights` in the table list). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched/created files. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Platform pages: inline run history, connection tests, query history, SQL highlighting

1. **Goal / behavior**: Implemented the six "Improve Platform section" To-Do sub-issues. **Workflows** — every workflow card now shows an inline status strip: last-run outcome (success/failure icon) + relative time, or "Never run", plus a total run-count badge — computed straight from data already being fetched for the Run History tab, no new request. **Connectors** — each source card whose provider is in the existing schema-discovery allow-list gets a **Test** button that runs a lightweight `SELECT 1` via `querySource` and toasts success/failure (logging to Error Log on failure); the Sources/Destinations type filter is now derived client-side from the single unfiltered fetch instead of firing a second near-duplicate request, and the AI/ML-vs-Data category request (which genuinely needs the server, since `category` isn't a field on the connector response) now only fires when a category filter is actually selected. **Query** — a new "Recent" tab tracks the last 15 run queries (per browser, localStorage) with Load/Save actions; the plain `Textarea` SQL editor was replaced with a new `SqlEditor` component giving live syntax highlighting while typing.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/DB change. `workflows.tsx` — `latestRunByWorkflowId`/`runCountByWorkflowId` memos derived from the existing `runs` query (already ordered `desc(createdAt)` server-side, so the first match per workflow id is its latest). `connectors.tsx` — exported `DISCOVERY_QUERIES` from `ConnectorDetailSheet.tsx` (previously module-private) and reused it both for the new "testable" check and the `SELECT 1` test call; added a second `useConnectors(..., { enabled: categoryActive })` call gated so it only runs when `typeFilter === "source" && categoryFilter !== "all"`. New `src/components/query/SqlEditor.tsx` — a Shiki (`codeToHtml`, theme `css-variables`, already a project dependency used by `CodeBlockCode`) highlighted `<div>` absolutely positioned behind a transparent-text `<textarea>` (the standard highlighted-editor overlay technique), with a `onScroll` handler keeping the two layers' scroll position in sync. `query.tsx` — local `RecentQuery[]` state persisted to `localStorage` (`query-recent-v1`, capped at 15, de-duplicated by connector+query, most-recent-first) pushed on every successful run; swapped its `Textarea` for `SqlEditor`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Recent Queries, like the To-Do/Settings localStorage fallbacks elsewhere in the app, is intentionally per-browser only (no DB table) — acceptable for a lightweight "recently run" convenience feature. The Test button only appears for providers already in `DISCOVERY_QUERIES` (`Postgresql`, `MariaDB`, `Oracle`) — same verified-live allow-list used for schema discovery.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Workflows' inline run stats only reflect runs recorded through this app's own "Run" dialog (`workflow_runs` table) — external platform-side runs aren't visible here, same limitation as the existing Run History tab. The connector Test button doesn't exist for destinations or for source providers outside the SQL allow-list (AI/ML sources, Snowflake, Databricks, etc.) — no button renders for those, rather than a disabled one, to avoid implying a capability that doesn't exist. `SqlEditor`'s highlight-overlay technique requires the overlay and textarea to share identical font/line-height/padding to stay visually aligned — both are hardcoded to match (`font-mono text-sm`, `lineHeight: "1.5"`, `p-3`) rather than computed, so a future className change to one must be mirrored in the other.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched/created files. Confirmed `/workflows`, `/connectors`, `/query` still return 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Dashboard: quick actions, combined activity queries, polished empty states

1. **Goal / behavior**: Implemented the three "Improve Dashboard section" To-Do sub-issues. (a) A Quick Actions row next to the page title — **New Task** (inline dialog, title only, posts to the same `todos` table the To-Do page uses), **New Content** (inline dialog, title + scheduled date, posts to `content_calendar_items`), **Run Workflow** (dialog listing registered workflows -> opens the existing `WorkflowRunDialog` to actually run one) — so the home page is no longer purely read-only. (b) The 5 separate client-side queries the page used to fire in parallel (todos, error logs, audit logs, storage files, workflow runs) are now one combined request. (c) The three "no data" states (Task Breakdown, Recent Activity, Latest Workflow Runs) now use the shared `Empty`/`EmptyHeader`/`EmptyMedia` components with a relevant icon instead of a bare `<p>`, and the Task/Workflow ones include a direct call-to-action button that opens the matching quick-action dialog.
2. **Data flow**: New `src/services/db/dashboardService.ts` — `getDashboardSummary({ data: { includeStorage } })` runs all 5 queries with `Promise.all` inside a single `createServerFn` handler (still 5 DB queries, but 1 HTTP round-trip instead of 5); `includeStorage` is passed from `useCapabilities().s3Enabled` so the storage query is skipped server-side when storage isn't configured, matching the previous `enabled: dbEnabled && s3Enabled` gating. `index.tsx` replaced its five `useQuery` calls with one (`queryKey: ["dashboardSummary", s3Enabled]`) and destructures `todos`/`errorLogs`/`auditLogs`/`storageFiles`/`workflowRuns` off the single response; a `summaryLoading` flag now backs all five per-card loading states (previously independent). Quick actions reuse existing services directly — `createTodo` (`todoService`), `createContentCalendarItem` (`contentCalendarService`), and `listRegisteredWorkflows` + the existing `WorkflowRunDialog` component (`@/components/workflows/WorkflowRunDialog`) — no new tables. Both create mutations also write an `audit_logs` row (category `todo`/`content`) and invalidate the relevant query on success (`dashboardSummary` for the task, `contentCalendarItems` for content so the Content Calendar page reflects it immediately).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. The workflow picker's list only loads when its dialog is open (`enabled: dbEnabled && quickRunOpen`) to avoid an unnecessary fetch on every dashboard load.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: The New Content quick-action only asks for title + date (no channel/status/description) — full editing still happens on `/content-calendar`. The Run Workflow quick action reuses `WorkflowRunDialog` as-is, so the same "must be published" run guard documented in earlier entries still applies. Combining the 5 queries into 1 request means they now share a single loading/error state instead of resolving independently (a page with a slow error-log query, for example, now delays the whole dashboard rather than just that one card) — an acceptable tradeoff given all 5 are small internal-DB reads.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only; fixed one real new error from an untyped `Select` `onValueChange` callback by casting to `string`, matching the existing project convention). `bunx biome format --write` on both touched/created files. Confirmed `/` still returns 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### To-Do: parent/sub-issue hierarchy

1. **Goal / behavior**: Tasks on `/todo` can now be nested under a parent task. The New Task form gained a "Parent task" picker (top-level tasks only, DB mode only), and sub-issues render indented under their parent with a dashed border and a "Sub-issue of #id title" badge; sub-issues are always ordered immediately after their parent in the list regardless of creation order. Used to log 9 parent "section" improvement issues (one per sidebar nav group) with 43 nested per-page sub-issues from an earlier page-by-page suggestions review.
2. **Data flow**: Added a self-referencing `todos.parent_id` column (`references(() => todos.id, { onDelete: "cascade" })`, typed via `AnyPgColumn` for the lazy self-reference) via migration `0013_uneven_norman_osborn.sql`. `todoService.ts`'s `TodoInput`/`createTodo` gained an optional `parentId`. `todo.tsx` — `topLevelTodos` memo (parentless tasks, for the picker), `todoById` memo (id -> task, to resolve a child's parent for the badge), and `filteredTodos` now reorders the status-filtered list so each parent is immediately followed by its own children (tasks whose `parentId` doesn't resolve to a task in the current list are treated as top-level, e.g. under a status filter that excludes the parent).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. The local-storage fallback mode (no DB) does not support hierarchy — the Parent Task picker only renders when `dbEnabled`.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Only one level of nesting is rendered/supported by the UI (a sub-issue itself could technically be given a `parentId` pointing at another sub-issue via direct DB manipulation, but the picker only offers top-level tasks, and rendering doesn't recurse beyond one level). Deleting a parent cascades to delete all its sub-issues (`onDelete: "cascade"`) — there's no "orphan the children" option.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `todos` now has 11 columns / 2 fks). Seed script inserted 9 parents + 43 sub-issues, logged. `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched files. Confirmed `/todo` still returns 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Sidebar polish + fixed overlapping global floating buttons

(b) Fixed a real UI bug: the "Theme active" floating badge (`ThemeApplier.tsx`) and the global search button (`GlobalSearch.tsx`) were both `fixed`, `z-50`, and anchored to the same bottom-right corner — they could visually stack/overlap whenever a custom theme was active. The theme badge is now anchored bottom-left instead; the search button stays bottom-right and got a subtle hover scale/shadow.

Only the two floating buttons already present were addressed; there's no other floating/global UI in the app currently.
