### Removed data source wizard; Data Dashboard and AI Insights are now schema-driven

1. **Goal / behavior**: (a) Fully removed the New Data Source Wizard and the underlying connector-request feature — the "New Data Source" button, `ConnectorWizardDialog`, the "Requests" tab, and the `connector_requests` table are all gone from `/connectors`, which is back to a plain browse-only page with copy pointing users to ask in the AppGen chat for a connector created immediately (that capability was never real live-creation — see the two entries above). (b) `/data-dashboard` no longer assumes an `employees`/`departments` schema (the user reported no employee data present) — it now discovers the connector's actual tables live, lets the user pick any one from a `Select`, and derives generic metric cards (row count, column count, an average of the first inferred numeric column) and a group-by bar chart (on the first inferred low-cardinality categorical column) purely from that table's real columns. (c) `/insights` replaced its hardcoded 6-table HR list with a live-discovered checkbox grid of every table in the connector — the user selects which ones to include, and the AI prompt is built generically from whichever tables were checked.
2. **Data flow**: No new tables (`ai_insights` from the earlier entry is unchanged). Schema change: dropped `connector_requests` (migration `0012_hot_firebrand.sql`) and removed its Drizzle definitions/types from `schema.ts`; deleted `src/services/db/connectorRequestService.ts` and `src/components/connectors/ConnectorWizardDialog.tsx` outright (not just unused — fully removed). `data-dashboard.tsx` — single `useQuerySource()`-backed flow: `information_schema.tables` discovery -> `SELECT * FROM <table> LIMIT 200` on selection change, with a local `analyzeColumns(rows)` helper that classifies each column as numeric (all-numeric values, excluding `*_id`/`id` columns) or categorical (2-12 distinct string values) from the fetched sample to drive the metric card and chart generically. `insights.tsx` — same `information_schema.tables` discovery on mount populates a `Checkbox` grid (`selectedTables: Set<string>`); "Generate Insights" loops the selected set with `querySource.mutateAsync` (LIMIT 200 each), serializes every row generically as `key: value` pairs (no more employee/department-specific formatting or join), and sends the combined summary to the same AI/ML connector (1803).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Both pages still hardcode `CONNECTOR_ID`/`DATA_CONNECTOR_ID = 2108` (the confirmed data source) and the insights page still hardcodes `MODEL_CONNECTOR_ID = 1803` — only the *table* selection within that connector is now dynamic, not the connector itself.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Generic column-type inference is heuristic (string-parseable-as-number = numeric, 2-12 distinct values = categorical) — tables with no low-cardinality text column won't get a breakdown chart, and tables with no clean numeric column fall back to showing a numeric-column count instead of an average; this is an acceptable tradeoff for working across arbitrary tables without per-table code. Both pages cap live fetches at `LIMIT 200` rows per table to keep queries and AI prompts bounded. The wizard's removal means there is now no in-app path at all to request a new connector — only asking directly in this chat.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `connector_requests` no longer in the table list). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched files. Confirmed `/data-dashboard`, `/insights`, `/connectors` still return 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Fix: data source wizard expectations, live schema picker, broader AI insights

1. **Goal / behavior**: (a) The New Data Source Wizard and the Connectors page's "Requests" tab now clearly state upfront that the app cannot create a live connector by itself — it queues a request for an admin, and if a connector is wanted immediately the user should ask for it directly in the AppGen chat (confirmed with the user as the intended workflow: this build agent can create it live via its own admin tools in the same conversation). (b) `/data-dashboard` gained an "Explore Table" card: a `Select` populated by a live `information_schema.tables` query against the connector, defaulting to the first table, that loads and renders `SELECT * FROM <table> LIMIT 100` generically (dynamic columns) whenever the selection changes or Refresh is clicked — so the dashboard isn't limited to the hardcoded employees/departments view. (c) `/insights` now queries and summarizes six tables (`employees`, `departments`, `benefits`, `employee_benefits`, `leave_balances`, `leave_requests`) sequentially via `querySource.mutateAsync` before prompting the model, instead of just `employees`/`departments`.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/DB change. `ConnectorWizardDialog.tsx` and `connectors.tsx` — copy-only edits (no logic change; `createConnectorRequest` still just inserts into `connector_requests`, unchanged). `data-dashboard.tsx` — new `loadAvailableTables()` (queries `information_schema.tables where table_schema = 'public'`) and `loadSelectedTable(table)` (interpolates the table name directly into the SQL string, but only ever with a value the query itself returned — never raw user text — so this isn't a user-controlled SQL-injection surface); both wired to a `useEffect` on mount and on `selectedTable` change, plus into the existing Refresh button. `insights.tsx` — replaced the two nested `querySource.mutate` calls with a `for...of` loop over `INSIGHT_TABLES` using `await querySource.mutateAsync(...)`, collecting results into a `Record<string, TableRow[]>` before building the model prompt; non-employee tables are serialized generically (`key: value` pairs) rather than with bespoke per-table formatting.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. `INSIGHT_TABLES` is a hardcoded allow-list (not the full discovered schema) because this connector also holds unrelated demo datasets (`drug_data`, `fortune1000`, `documents`, `performance_records`, vector-embedding tables) that would dilute/derail HR insights — confirmed via live `aisquared_connectors_discover`/`connectors_query_source` during the original build.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Still no live connector-creation path from inside the running app (by design/platform limitation, now stated explicitly in the UI rather than implied) — the only way to get a connector created immediately is to ask in the AppGen chat, not through the in-app wizard. The dashboard's table picker is generic (no per-table chart/formatting beyond the existing employees/departments cards) — new tables just render as a raw column/row table. Insight table selection is still a fixed list, not user-configurable from the UI.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all four touched files. Confirmed `/data-dashboard`, `/insights`, `/connectors` still return 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Expanded Connectors schema discovery to Oracle

1. **Goal / behavior**: "Discover Schema" on `/connectors` now also works for Oracle sources, not just Postgres/MariaDB. The empty-state copy was updated to mention Oracle.
2. **Data flow**: `ConnectorDetailSheet.tsx` — replaced the single hardcoded `DISCOVERY_QUERY` (Postgres-style `information_schema.columns`) with a `DISCOVERY_QUERIES` map keyed by `connector_name`, each holding the catalog query that engine actually understands: Postgresql/MariaDB keep `information_schema.columns`; Oracle uses `ALL_TAB_COLUMNS` (Oracle has no `information_schema`). `canDiscover`/`handleDiscover` now look up the query for the connector's `connector_name` instead of checking a plain allow-list `Set`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Re-verified live via the `aisquared` MCP tools before expanding: Oracle's `ALL_TAB_COLUMNS` query returned real rows. Snowflake, Databricks, ClickHouse, and AWS Athena were re-tested too but still don't work generically right now — the live test connectors for Snowflake (missing ODBC driver) and Databricks (platform `BAD_REQUEST`) errored outright; BigQuery's `information_schema.columns` needs a specific dataset/region qualifier that isn't known generically; ClickHouse's test connector failed even on `SELECT 1`. These stay excluded until a live connector actually proves the query out, per the existing project convention of verifying against real connectors rather than guessing syntax.
5. **Validation**: Verified the new Oracle query directly against a live Oracle connector via `aisquared_connectors_query_source` before wiring it up (real column rows returned). Re-verified Postgresql/MariaDB still work. `bunx biome format --write` on the touched file. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Audit: prevent accidental table wipes + fix blank chat empty state

1. **Goal / behavior**: Audited every database-backed page for two things: (a) destructive actions that clear a whole table without confirmation, (b) pages that render nothing (blank) instead of a proper empty state when their table is empty. Found and fixed two real issues: the Workflows page's "Clear History" button deleted the entire `workflow_runs` table on a single click with zero confirmation; the AI Chat message pane showed a blank box for a brand-new conversation with no messages yet.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. `workflows.tsx` — wrapped the "Clear History" button in an `AlertDialog` (same component/pattern already used by `audit.tsx`'s "Clear All"), so `clearWorkflowRuns` only runs after the user confirms. `chat.tsx` — added a `uiMessages.length === 0` branch in the message pane (between the loading-skeleton branch and the message-list branch) showing "Send a message to start the conversation." instead of an empty `<div className="flex flex-col">`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Every other database-backed page/component was checked and already had a correct `.length === 0` → `<Empty>` (or text-fallback) branch: To-Do, Changelog, Audit Log, Error Log, Inventory, Storage, Gallery, Documents, the three Settings panels (API Keys/Webhooks/Integrations), Workflows' own registry list, Connectors' request list, Query's saved-queries/models tabs, and Theme's saved-themes grid — none of those needed changes. Content Calendar is a month-grid shell (not a linear list), so an empty month still renders a full, non-blank calendar by design. Other bulk-delete-without-confirmation calls that already had a confirmation dialog wired up correctly were left alone (e.g. Audit Log's own "Clear All").
5. **Validation**: Read every listed page/component's rendering logic before concluding it was already correct (no blind pattern-matching). `bunx biome format --write` on both touched files. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Five new features: global search, dashboard workflow runs, To-Do↔Changelog links, CSV export, document tags/search

1. **Goal / behavior**: (a) A floating search button (bottom-right, also `Cmd/Ctrl+K`) opens a command palette listing every registered page — pick one to navigate there. (b) Dashboard gained a "Latest Workflow Runs" card alongside the existing Tasks/Errors/Storage/Recent Activity cards. (c) To-Do items can optionally link to a Changelog entry (picker in the task's "Show details" panel); linked tasks show a "View in Changelog" chip and linked entries show a "View linked task" chip back. (d) Inventory, Audit Log, and the Query page's results table each got a "Download CSV" button that exports exactly what's on screen (respecting current filters). (e) Documents can have free-form tags and optional pasted "searchable content" text (via a new per-row Tag icon action); the Documents search box now matches filename, tags, or that content.
2. **Data flow**: New `src/components/search/GlobalSearch.tsx` (mounted once in `_protected.tsx`) reuses the existing `flattenNavEntries(routes)` utility — no new data source, purely client-side filtering via the shared `Command`/`CommandDialog` primitives. Dashboard (`index.tsx`) added a `listWorkflowRuns()` query (existing `workflowRegistryService`) — no schema change. To-Do↔Changelog: added `todos.changelog_entry_id` (nullable FK → `changelog_entries.id`, `onDelete: "set null"`); `todoService.ts` gained `updateTodoChangelogLink`; `todo.tsx` renders a `Select` of changelog entries in the task's detail panel, `changelog.tsx` fetches `listTodos()` and shows a link chip for entries that have at least one linked task. CSV export: new shared `src/lib/csvExport.ts` (`downloadCsv(filename, rows, columns?)`) building a CSV Blob and triggering a browser download client-side — no server endpoint. Document tags/search: added `storage_files.tags` (`text[]`) and `storage_files.extracted_text` (`text`, nullable); new `storageService.updateStorageFileMeta`; `documents.tsx` adds a tag/content-editing `Dialog` per row and extends its existing client-side filter to also check `tags`/`extractedText`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Migration `0008_smooth_celestials.sql` adds the two new columns; ran into the same `drizzle.__drizzle_migrations` empty-tracking-table issue documented in earlier entries (tables already existed in the DB but weren't tracked) — fixed the same way: backfilled hash/timestamp rows for the 8 already-applied migrations from `meta/_journal.json` before re-running `db:migrate` for migration 0008 only. No data was dropped.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: "Full-text search" on Documents is manual, not automatic OCR/extraction — there's no text-extraction pipeline in this template, so users paste/type the searchable content themselves (tags + content are both optional). The To-Do↔Changelog link is one-directional in storage (FK lives on `todos`) but shown bidirectionally in the UI by cross-querying both tables client-side; deleting a Changelog entry sets any linked To-Do's link to null rather than blocking the delete. CSV export is client-side only (no row-limit enforcement beyond whatever's already loaded on screen). The global search list is exactly the sidebar's nav entries (no fuzzy full-text search of page contents, just page titles).
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + migration-tracking backfill + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed new columns via re-running status; no new tables, so table list unchanged). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on every touched file. Confirmed `/`, `/todo`, `/changelog`, `/documents`, `/inventory`, `/audit`, `/query` all still return 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Theme Generator: Surprise Me, accent colors, noise texture, background animation

1. **Goal / behavior**: Added four enhancements to `/theme`: (a) a "Surprise Me" button that randomizes every field (gradient colors/direction, overlay tint, intensity/overlay, 2-3 accent colors, noise amount, animation) in one click; (b) up to 4 accent hex colors per theme, shown as swatches on the builder preview and saved-theme cards — the first one becomes the app's live `--accent` CSS variable while the theme is active, so real buttons/badges pick it up; (c) a noise/texture overlay slider (film-grain SVG, capped subtle); (d) a background animation picker (None/Floating Glow/Pulse/Slow Drift/Aurora Shift) with live CSS-animation preview in the builder and applied app-wide when active.
2. **Data flow**: Added `custom_themes.accent_colors` (`text[]`, nullable), `noise_opacity` (`integer`, default 0), `animation` (`text`, `$type<"none"|"float"|"pulse"|"drift"|"aurora">`, default `"none"`) via migration `0009_lumpy_vertigo.sql`. `src/lib/customTheme.ts` — extended `CustomTheme`, added `ThemeAnimation`/`ANIMATION_LABEL`, and `NOISE_TEXTURE_URI` (an inline `feTurbulence` SVG data URI, no image asset). `src/services/db/themeService.ts` — `CustomThemeInput` gained the three optional fields, defaulted server-side (`[]`/`0`/`"none"`) on insert. `theme.tsx` — new state (`accentColors`, `noiseOpacity`, `animation`), `handleSurpriseMe`/`addAccentColor`/`updateAccentColor`/`removeAccentColor`, new controls in the Effects tab, and the noise/animation/accent rendering mirrored in the live preview `<div>`. `ThemeApplier.tsx` — renders the noise layer (`mix-blend-overlay`) and animation class (`theme-anim-*`) on the fixed background layer, and sets `--theme-accent-1/2/3` + `--accent` on `<html>` when a theme with accent colors is active (cleared on deactivate/unmount). `src/styles.css` — added the four `@keyframes`/`.theme-anim-*` rules shared by both the live preview and `ThemeApplier`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Animations are intentionally subtle (5-16s durations, capped filter/opacity/scale ranges) to stay usable as an app background, not a distraction. Noise opacity is capped at 0.25 even at the 100% slider position — it reads as texture, not visible static. Overriding `--accent` only recolors components already using `bg-accent`/`text-accent-foreground` (per the existing `custom-theme-active` CSS-variable-override pattern already used for `--card`/`--sidebar`/etc.) — it does not touch `--primary`, so primary buttons keep their default color regardless of a theme's accent colors.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `custom_themes` now has 13 columns, no tracking-table issue this time). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only; fixed one real new error by making `CustomTheme.accentColors` nullable to match Drizzle's inferred `string[] | null`). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched files. Confirmed `/theme` still returns 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Cross-cutting: shared activity-log hook, standardized empty states, recently visited sidebar

1. **Goal / behavior**: Implemented the three "Improve cross-cutting concerns" To-Do sub-issues. (a) A new shared `useActivityLog()` hook replaces ~25 duplicated inline `createErrorLog`/`createAuditLog` call sites across the app with two stable callbacks (`logError`, `logAudit`). (b) Standardized "loading"/"no data yet" states: Data Dashboard's "no tables"/"no rows" and AI Insights' "no tables" plain-text/Alert states now use the shared `<Empty>` component (matching the rest of the app); Performance's "no samples yet"/"no resource entries yet" (previously plain text with no icon, on a page with no `<Empty>` usage at all) now do too. (c) The sidebar (`AppSidebar`) now shows a "Recently Visited" section above the main nav, listing up to 5 distinct pages the user has recently opened (excluding the current page), with the same icon/title as their main nav entry.
2. **Data flow**: New `src/hooks/useActivityLog.ts` wraps `createErrorLog`/`createAuditLog` (from the existing `errorLogService`/`auditLogService`) with the `.catch(() => {})` fire-and-forget pattern baked in; every route/component that previously imported those services directly (`inventory.tsx`, `content-calendar.tsx`, `storage.tsx`, `todo.tsx`, `index.tsx`, `connectors.tsx`, `data-dashboard.tsx`, `query.tsx`, `insights.tsx`, `ai-summariser.tsx`, `chat.tsx`, `settings.tsx`, `ConnectorDetailSheet.tsx`, `WorkflowRunDialog.tsx`) now calls `useActivityLog()` once and uses `logError`/`logAudit` instead — no schema/service change. Empty-state fixes are presentation-only (swapped `<p>`/`<Alert>` for `<Empty><EmptyHeader><EmptyMedia>...`). New `src/hooks/useRecentPages.ts` reads/writes a capped, deduped path list to `localStorage` (`sidebar-recent-pages-v1`, same pattern as the existing `sidebar-collapsed-groups-v1` key) keyed off `useRouterState().location.pathname`; `app-sidebar.tsx` resolves those paths back to `{title, url, icon}` via the existing `flattenNavEntries(routes)` utility and renders them as a `SidebarGroup` above the routes loop.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config, no schema change. "Recently Visited" is per-browser only (localStorage), like the To-Do/Settings/Query "Recent" localStorage fallbacks documented elsewhere in this file.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: A handful of small in-card chart-empty-state placeholders (`analytics.tsx`'s per-chart "No tasks recorded yet." etc., `theme.tsx`'s "No accent colors yet"/"No images found") were deliberately left as lightweight inline text rather than converted to the full `<Empty>` component — they're internal to a small card slot where the icon+title+description layout would look oversized, and each file is internally consistent with itself. `error-log.tsx`'s own direct `createErrorLog` call (letting a user manually add a test log entry) was left as-is since it's the log page itself, not a duplicated pattern. "Recently Visited" only tracks pages already registered in `src/components/layout/index.ts`'s `routes` array — visiting an unregistered/dynamic route won't resolve to a title/icon and is silently skipped from the list.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome check`/`format --write` on every touched file — no new lint errors introduced (pre-existing `noExplicitAny`/`useParseIntRadix`/`noArrayIndexKey` warnings in files we touched were already present, unrelated to these changes). Confirmed `/`, `/inventory`, and other touched routes still return 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v1.9.0) and marked To-Do items #88 (parent), #89, #90, #91 as done. Did not run `bun run build`/full `bun run check` per project rules.

### Sidebar: color-coded nav sections

1. **Goal / behavior**: Every sidebar section (including the "Recently Visited" group added above) now has its own distinct color — label text, page icons, and the active-page left-border accent all use that section's color — so sections are visually distinguishable from each other at a glance, not just by their text label.
2. **Data flow**: No data/schema change — pure component styling. `app-sidebar.tsx` — added a fixed `GROUP_COLORS` palette (blue/violet/emerald/sky/fuchsia/rose Tailwind color pairs: label+icon text color, active-border color) and a `groupColor(index)` helper that cycles through it; the `routes.map` loop now tracks a separate `groupIndex` counter (incremented only for actual `NavGroup` entries, so ungrouped standalone items like Dashboard/Theme/Settings are skipped and keep the neutral/primary color) and passes the resolved color into the group label, each item's icon, and the `data-active:border-*` class. The pre-existing "Recently Visited" section keeps its own hardcoded amber color rather than pulling from the cycle, since it's not a `routes` entry.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Colors are assigned by a group's position in the `routes` array, not by a stable per-label mapping — reordering or inserting a new group in `src/components/layout/index.ts` will shift every subsequent group's color. With 6 palette colors and 6 current groups there's no repeat yet, but a 7th group would repeat the first color.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome check`/`format --write` on the touched file — no new lint errors (same pre-existing `noArrayIndexKey` warnings as before, unchanged). Confirmed `/` still returns 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server.

### To-Do: separated Done tasks into their own collapsible section

1. **Goal / behavior**: On the To-Do page's "All" filter view, open tasks (pending/in progress) now always render at the top; completed tasks are grouped into a collapsible "Done" section below (with a count badge and expand/collapse toggle) instead of being interleaved by creation order. The Pending/In Progress/Done filter buttons are unaffected — selecting a single status still shows a plain flat list as before.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/service change. `todo.tsx` — the existing parent/sub-issue ordering helper (`orderWithSubIssues`) was moved inside a single `useMemo` that now returns three lists: `filteredTodos` (unchanged behavior, used for the non-"all" filters and the empty-state check), `openTodos` (non-done tasks, `filter === "all"` only), and `doneTodos` (done tasks, `filter === "all"` only) — each independently ordered so parent/sub-issue adjacency is preserved within its own group. The card JSX itself was extracted into a `renderTodoCard(todo)` function (previously inline in a single `.map`) so it can be reused for both the flat list and the two new sections without duplicating markup. New `doneSectionOpen` state (component-local, not persisted) drives the `Collapsible` around the Done list, defaulting to expanded.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: A sub-issue's own status can differ from its parent's — a done sub-issue under a still-open parent stays in the Done section separately from its parent (still shows its "Sub-issue of #id title" badge for context), rather than being kept adjacent to the parent card. `doneSectionOpen` is per-session only (resets to expanded on page reload) since it wasn't asked to persist.

Sorting a parent task by priority/due-date can separate it from its own sub-issues in the raw sort order; the existing `orderWithSubIssues` step (applied after sort) still re-groups each parent with its children immediately after it, so hierarchy is preserved but a sub-issue's individual sort position is subordinated to its parent's.

### Fix: To-Do Kanban cards rendered poorly in narrow columns

1. **Goal / behavior**: Task cards inside the Kanban view's three columns no longer look cramped/overlapping — they now use a compact layout suited to a ~1/3-page-width column instead of reusing the List view's wide horizontal card as-is.

For `variant === "kanban"`: the `Card` switches from `flex-row` to `flex-col`; the priority-color bar switches from a left side strip (`w-1` full height) to a top stripe (`h-1` full width); `CardContent` padding drops the asymmetric list padding for a uniform `p-3`; the sub-issue indent (`ml-8` in List) is dropped entirely (a narrow column has no room for it — the existing "Sub-issue of #id title" badge already conveys the relationship, now with a `truncate` span so a long parent title can't blow out the column width); the "Show details" collapsible and its description/solution panel drop the `ml-[30px]` indent and the `sm:grid-cols-2` two-column split, rendering description then solution stacked in one column instead of side-by-side. The three Kanban column call sites now pass `renderTodoCard(todo, "kanban")` instead of `renderTodoCard(todo)`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Kanban sub-issue cards no longer visually nest under their parent via indentation (List view still does) — they rely solely on the "Sub-issue of #id title" badge for that context, which is a deliberate width tradeoff for the column layout.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome check`/`format --write` on the touched file — no new lint errors (same pre-existing `noExplicitAny` warning as before, unchanged). A new status dropdown next to the search box lets the user switch to "All statuses" or "Draft" to see everything or just drafts.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/service change. `workflows.tsx` — new `statusFilter` state (`"published" | "all" | "draft"`, defaulting to `"published"`) and a `STATUS_FILTERS` config array (same pattern as other filter dropdowns in the app); the `filtered` memo now filters `workflows` by `w.status === statusFilter` before applying the existing search-term filter (skipped entirely when `statusFilter === "all"`). The summary cards (Connected/Published/Total Runs counts) and the Run History tab are unaffected — they still reflect *all* registered workflows regardless of this list filter.

4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Only `"published"`/`"draft"` are handled as real filter values (the only two statuses the seed data and `registerWorkflow` ever set) — a workflow with any other status string would simply not match either the "published" or "draft" filter and would only show up under "All statuses".
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome check`/`format --write` on the touched file — no new lint issues. Confirmed `/workflows` still returns 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v1.12.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done.

### Fix: Workflows page showed "No published workflows" — registry was never seeded

1. **Goal / behavior**: `/workflows` no longer shows an empty state under the (now-default) Published filter — it correctly lists the workspace's 6 currently-published workflows (plus 3 drafts visible under "Draft"/"All statuses").
2. **Data flow**: No code/logic bug in the previous entry's filter — `workflow_registry` (the DB table the page reads from) was completely empty in this app's database; `seed-workflows.ts` had never actually been run against it. Separately, the script's hardcoded `WORKFLOWS` snapshot was stale: it had 16 entries from an earlier point in time, but the live workspace (verified via the `aisquared_workflows_list` MCP tool) now has only 9 workflows — 7 of the old snapshot's entries no longer exist in the platform at all. Trimmed `seed-workflows.ts`'s `WORKFLOWS` array down to exactly those 9 (matching each one's live `id`/`status`/`description` — see the file for the authoritative current list, 6 `published` / 3 `draft`), then ran `bun run src/server/db/seed-workflows.ts` (idempotent via `onConflictDoNothing`) to populate the table.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses the existing `DATABASE_URL`.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Same limitation called out in the original Workflows-page build entry — there's no runtime API to list workflows, so this registry is a manually-maintained snapshot that must be re-seeded (or edited directly in `seed-workflows.ts`, or via the page's own Add/Remove) whenever workflows are added, renamed, or deleted in the platform; it will not auto-sync.
5. **Validation**: Verified the live workflow list (ids, names, statuses, descriptions) directly via `aisquared_workflows_list` before rewriting the seed data — matched exactly (6 published / 3 draft) after reseeding, confirmed by querying `workflow_registry` directly. `bunx biome format --write` on the touched seed file. Confirmed `/workflows` still returns 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v1.12.1) and a To-Do item documenting the fix, marked done.

### Fix: page content got cut off instead of scrolling (Dashboard "Quick Access")

1. **Goal / behavior**: Reported as the Dashboard's "Quick Access" shortcut row (near the very bottom of the page) getting cut off — on any page taller than the viewport, content past the visible area was clipped rather than reachable by scrolling. Now every page in the app scrolls normally when its content exceeds the viewport height.
2. **Data flow**: Not a route-level bug — this is a shared layout bug in `src/components/layout/sidebar-layout.tsx` (a local, app-owned copy of the base `SidebarLayout`, not the read-only `/base` one — confirmed by diffing the two; only formatting differs, so the bug also exists in the base version but this app's own copy is the one actually rendered and safe to fix here). The wrapper `<div>` around `AppHeader` + `<main>` had `h-[calc(100vh-48px)]` but was missing `flex flex-col` — without a flex container, `<main>`'s `flex-1` had no effect and `overflow-auto` never got a bounded height to scroll within, so tall page content (like the Dashboard's Quick Access section) just overflowed past the div's bottom instead of scrolling into view. Added `flex flex-col` to that wrapper so `<main>` now correctly fills the remaining height below the header and scrolls its own content.
3. **Inputs/config**: No env/config change.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: This fixes scrolling app-wide (every page renders through this same shared layout), not just the Dashboard — no page-specific change was needed beyond this one shared-layout class fix.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on the touched file. Confirmed `/` still returns 307 (auth redirect, no 500) from the dev server. Playwright browser verification was unavailable in this environment (Chromium not installed) — relied on reading the layout's CSS/flex logic directly instead, per project rules for environment/browser errors. Added a Changelog entry (v1.12.2) and a To-Do item documenting the fix, marked done.

### Fix: found the real cause of data loss on republish + added Settings → Backups (backup/restore)

1. **Goal / behavior**: (a) Root-caused the reported "development data disappears on republish" issue: it was not a dev-vs-published database split (there is only one persistent Neon database, confirmed via the provisioning tool), but the database's own migration-tracking table being out of sync with reality. (b) Added a safety net regardless of cause: a new **Backups** tab on `/settings` lets the user snapshot every table to storage on demand and restore any past snapshot back into the database.
2. **Data flow**: (a) No app code changed for the root-cause fix — it was a one-time data repair. `drizzle.__drizzle_migrations` was empty even though all 14 migrations' tables already existed with real data; running `bun run db:migrate` in that state throws `relation already exists` trying to replay migration `0000` from scratch (confirmed by reproducing it live while adding the new `db_backups` table). Fixed by computing the sha256 hash of each already-applied migration file (same algorithm `drizzle-orm/migrator.js` uses: `sha256(fileContents)`) and inserting a matching `(hash, created_at)` row per `meta/_journal.json` entry, so Drizzle now correctly recognizes migrations `0000`-`0013` as applied; `db:migrate` then only applied the genuinely new `0014` migration. (b) New `db_backups` table (`src/server/db/schema.ts`, migration `0014_burly_unicorn.sql`) stores backup metadata (`key`, `tableCount`, `rowCount`, `sizeBytes`, `status`, `createdAt`) since the S3 helper API has no "list objects" call — the DB row is what lets the app list/restore backups. New `src/services/db/backupService.ts`: `createBackup()` reads every app table (a fixed `BACKUP_TABLE_NAMES` list covering all Drizzle-defined tables except `db_backups` itself), bundles them into one JSON snapshot, uploads it to `exports/backups/<timestamp>-backup.json` via `uploadFile`, and inserts a `db_backups` row; `listBackups()` reads that table; `restoreBackup(id)` downloads the snapshot via `downloadFile`, strips each row's original `id` (so restored rows get fresh identity values instead of colliding with existing ones), and re-inserts into each table. New `src/components/settings/BackupsPanel.tsx` (same list/empty-state/skeleton pattern as `ApiKeysPanel`/`WebhooksPanel`) renders a "Backup now" button, a table of past backups, and a per-row "Restore" action gated behind an `AlertDialog` confirmation (since it's an additive insert, not a destructive action, but still worth confirming). Wired into `settings.tsx` as a new "Backups" tab, gated on `useCapabilities().databaseEnabled`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses the existing `DATABASE_URL` and provisioned S3 storage. Backup snapshots live under `exports/backups/` (app-generated reports, matching the existing `exports/` folder convention).
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Restore is additive-only by design — it never deletes/overwrites existing rows, so restoring an old backup on top of current data can create duplicates (called out explicitly in the confirmation dialog's copy); a "replace all data" restore mode was deliberately not built since it would be genuinely destructive. Restore does not remap foreign keys between backed-up tables (e.g. a restored `chat_message.conversation_id` still points at the *old* conversation id, which may now belong to a different row after re-insertion with fresh identity values) — acceptable for a disaster-recovery safety net, not a guaranteed perfect merge. `db_backups` itself is intentionally excluded from its own snapshots. This fix does not (and cannot) change how the platform's publish pipeline wires environments — that remains outside what any tool available to the coding agent can inspect or configure; the actionable fix was repairing the desynced migration state, which is a real, verifiable bug in this app's database.
5. **Validation**: Reproduced the `relation already exists` migration failure live, diagnosed it by querying `drizzle.__drizzle_migrations` directly (found it empty) and `information_schema.tables` (found the tables already there), then repaired it and confirmed `bun run db:migrate` completes cleanly afterward with all existing data intact (`bun run db:status` — table list unchanged plus new `db_backups`; verified table row counts before/after). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only; fixed two real new errors — an unused `Badge` import and a zero-arg `createServerFn` call needing an explicit `()` wrapper). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched/created files. Confirmed `/settings` returns 200 from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v2.2.0, v2.2.1) and two To-Do items documenting the root-cause fix and the new backup feature, both marked done.

### Monitoring & Logs section: comparison/export on Analytics, pagination on Audit/Error Log, thresholds on Performance, filters/index on Changelog

1. **Goal / behavior**: Implemented the four remaining "Monitoring & Logs section improvements" To-Do sub-issues. **Analytics** — a "Compare to previous period" switch shows a card with Activity events/Errors logged/Tasks created for the selected range vs the immediately preceding equal-length period (delta arrow + %); every chart card (Activity over time, Tasks by status/priority, Inventory by category, Errors by severity) gained an "export as PNG" icon button alongside the existing CSV export. **Audit Log** and **Error Log** — both tables are now paginated (25 rows/page, Previous/Next + range indicator), applied after existing search/category/severity/status filters; changing any filter resets to page 1. **Performance** — a new "Alert Thresholds" popover (min FPS, max heap MB, max LCP ms, persisted to `localStorage`) drives a destructive banner listing every currently-breached metric, highlights the corresponding KPI card, and fires a one-time toast per newly-crossed threshold (cleared once the metric recovers). **Changelog** — type filter chips (Feature/Fix/Improvement/Breaking/Security, multi-select) filter the timeline to matching entries (hiding version groups left empty); a sticky "Jump to version" index next to the timeline scrolls to any version's section.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. `analyticsService.ts` — new `getAnalyticsComparison(range)` computes current-window vs previous-window counts for `auditLogs`/`errorLogs`/`todos` via two `gte`+`lt` date-range queries per table (6 total), run in parallel. New `src/lib/chartExport.ts` (`exportChartAsPng(container, filename)`) finds the first `<svg>` inside a ref'd container, clones it, resolves any `var(--chart-N)` CSS custom properties to concrete colors inline (recharts/shadcn charts rely on ancestor-scoped CSS vars that wouldn't resolve in an isolated serialized SVG), rasterizes via an offscreen `<canvas>` at 2x scale, and triggers a download — no new dependency. `audit.tsx`/`error-log.tsx` — pagination is a pure client-side `useMemo` slice over the already-filtered array (both pages already fetch the full table and filter client-side; this only changes what's *rendered*, not what's fetched, consistent with the existing architecture). `performance.tsx` — thresholds/breaches are derived client-side from the existing live-sampled metrics state (`fps`/`heapUsed`/`lcp`), no server round-trip. `changelog.tsx` — `groups` (the existing version-grouping logic) now derives from a new `typeFilter` state via `.filter`/`.map`, and each version's timeline `<div>` got a `id="changelog-version-{version}"` anchor that the jump-index's buttons scroll to via `scrollIntoView`.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Performance's alert thresholds are per-browser (`localStorage` key `performance-alert-thresholds-v1`), matching the page's existing all-client-side, no-database design (browser performance metrics never leave the device). PNG export re-reads `getComputedStyle(document.documentElement)` at click time, so it always reflects the currently active theme/dark-mode colors, not stale values.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Audit/Error Log pagination is still a single full-table fetch under the hood (no server-side `LIMIT`/`OFFSET`) — this reduces rendered DOM size on large logs but not network/query cost; a true server-paginated version would need `listAuditLogs`/`listErrorLogs` to accept page params, which was out of scope here since filtering is also entirely client-side today. The PNG export's CSS-variable resolution only handles `fill`/`stroke` attributes and inline `style` strings on chart children — a chart using CSS-var colors through a Tailwind class (rather than recharts' own `fill`/`stroke` props) wouldn't be caught, though none of the current charts do this. Performance thresholds only cover FPS/heap/LCP (the three metrics with clear "lower/higher is worse" semantics and existing rating logic) — FCP/TTFB/navigation-timing metrics don't have configurable thresholds yet.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched/created files. Confirmed `/analytics`, `/audit`, `/error-log`, `/performance`, `/changelog` all return 200 from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v2.4.0) and marked To-Do items #100 (parent), #101, #102, #103, #104 done.

### To-Do: completed date, created/completed date filters, full changelog linking

1. **Goal / behavior**: Task cards on `/todo` now show both a "Created" date and (once marked Done) a "Completed" date. A new "Date filters" popover lets the user filter the list by a created-date range and/or a completed-date range independently. Every existing Done task is now linked to a Changelog entry — two previously-undocumented fixes (Workflows inline run history/status badges, Sidebar recently-visited pages) got retroactive Changelog entries created so no Done task is left without a link.
2. **Data flow**: Added `todos.completed_at` (nullable timestamp) via migration `0015_adorable_patch.sql`. `todoService.ts` — `createTodo` sets it if a task is created already `done`; `updateTodoStatus` sets it to `now()` when transitioning to `done` and clears it (`null`) for any other status. `todo.tsx` — new `createdFrom`/`createdTo`/`completedFrom`/`completedTo` state feed a new `isWithinDateRange(value, from, to)` helper (inclusive day-level comparison against "YYYY-MM-DD" date-input strings) applied inside the existing `visibleTodos` memo, alongside the priority filter and before sorting; the local-storage fallback path (`handleCreate`/`handleCycleStatus` for when the DB is off) mirrors the same `completedAt` set/clear behavior client-side. One-time data repair: backfilled `completed_at` for all 43 already-done tasks using their `updated_at` as the best available estimate (no better source existed), then linked every one of them to a `changelog_entries` row — 41 matched an existing entry by content, and 2 new entries were inserted (v2.4.1 "Workflows: inline run history and status badges", v2.4.2 "Sidebar: recently visited pages") for fixes that had been implemented earlier but never got a formal Changelog row.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Date filter inputs are plain `<Input type="date">` pairs (not a calendar-range picker component) — consistent with the existing `dueDate` field's own input style on this page.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: `completed_at` for the 43 backfilled tasks is an estimate (`updated_at` at the time of the repair), not the task's true original completion moment, since that information was never captured before this change — going forward it will be accurate for every new completion. The created/completed date filters combine with AND logic across both ranges and the existing priority filter; there's no OR/either-field mode.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (table list unchanged, column added). Verified via direct query that all 43 Done tasks now have both `completed_at` and `changelog_entry_id` set (zero unlinked). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched files. Confirmed `/todo` returns 200 from the dev server. Added a Changelog entry (v2.5.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done.

### Removed sample data; added a CSV Export page (`/export`)

1. **Goal / behavior**: Removed leftover sample/demo data across the app: the To-Do page's hardcoded 3-task fallback (previously seeded into `localStorage` when the database is off) is gone — it now starts empty; the unused "Seed Data" page/service and several one-off dev scripts that inserted fake Changelog/To-Do rows (`seed.ts`, `seed-today.ts`, `seed-remaining-pages.ts`, `seed-inventory.ts`, `seed-inventory-samples.ts`, `seedRecentChanges.ts`) were deleted outright. Added a new `/export` page (Files & Media nav group) with one card per dataset — To-Do, Changelog, Audit Log, Error Log — each with an "Export to Storage" button that generates a CSV and uploads it to the `exports/` folder in Storage, plus a "Saved Exports" table with download/delete actions for past exports.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change (reuses the existing `storage_files` table, filtered to `folder = "exports"` and `.csv` filenames, to list past exports — no new table). New `src/lib/csvExport.ts` export `rowsToCsv()` — the pure CSV-string-building logic extracted out of the existing client-only `downloadCsv()`, now shared so it can run on the server too. New `src/services/db/exportService.ts`: `exportDataset(dataset)` reads the matching table (`todos`/`changelogEntries`/`auditLogs`/`errorLogs`) with Drizzle, builds a CSV via `rowsToCsv`, uploads it with `uploadFile` to `exports/<name>-<timestamp>.csv`, and inserts a `storage_files` row; `listDataExports()`, `getDataExportUrl()`, `deleteDataExport()` mirror the existing `storageService.ts` patterns. `export.tsx` is gated on `useCapabilities()` (`databaseEnabled` && `s3Enabled`) and logs each export via `useActivityLog().logAudit` (category `export`).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses `DATABASE_URL` and the already-provisioned S3 storage. Note: `seed-workflows.ts` and `seed-models.ts` were deliberately **kept** (not removed) — they back live features (the Workflows registry and Query page's Models tab, both documented in earlier entries above) rather than being sample/demo data, and deleting them would break those pages.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Export files are generated fresh on click (not scheduled/automatic) and are a point-in-time snapshot — re-running "Export to Storage" creates a new timestamped file rather than overwriting the previous one, so the exports list can accumulate over time; users delete old ones manually from the Saved Exports table. CSV export has no row-count cap (unlike some other pages' `LIMIT 200` live-data queries) since these tables are small internal logs, not third-party datasets.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched/created files. Confirmed the To-Do page's fallback change doesn't affect DB-backed mode. Added a Changelog entry (v2.6.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done.

### Renamed Export -> Data Manager (`/data-manager`); added "Re-add from Exports"

1. **Goal / behavior**: The Export page is renamed **Data Manager** (route moved `/export` -> `/data-manager`, sidebar entry updated to match) since it now does more than export. Added a **"Re-add from Exports"** button (confirmation-gated) that reads the most recent To-Do, Changelog, Audit Log, and Error Log CSVs saved in the `exports/` storage folder and re-inserts any rows currently missing from the database, skipping ones that already exist.
2. **Data flow**: New `src/lib/csvImport.ts` — a small hand-rolled CSV parser (`parseCsv`, handling quoted/escaped fields matching `rowsToCsv`'s output) plus type-coercion helpers (`csvValueToNullableString/Int/Date`). New `reimportFromExports()` server function in `exportService.ts`: uses the app-owned `listAllBucketObjects()` (added in the Storage "all folders" feature) to find every `exports/*.csv`, picks the most recently modified file per dataset (matched by filename prefix `todos-`/`changelog-`/`audit-log-`/`error-log-`), downloads+parses each with `downloadFile`, and inserts missing rows. Changelog is processed **first** (deduped by matching `version`+`title` against the live table) so its old-CSV-id -> new-real-id map is ready before To-Dos are processed; To-Dos are deduped by title, then a second pass remaps each restored row's `parentId`/`changelogEntryId` from the old CSV ids to the newly-inserted real ids (a parent can appear before or after its child in the CSV). Audit/Error Logs are deduped by exact `action`+`resource`+timestamp / `message`+timestamp (append-only logs, no FK to remap).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; still dev-only (`requireDevEnvironment()`, matching the rest of this page). Only "re-adds", never deletes/overwrites — safe to click repeatedly.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Only covers the four datasets this page already exports (To-Do/Changelog/Audit Log/Error Log) — other tables (Inventory, Settings panels, Theme presets, Chat, Content Calendar, Saved Queries, AI Insights, Storage file metadata) have no export/backup path today and can't be restored this way (Settings -> Backups' full-table JSON snapshot is the only thing that would cover those, if one exists). Dedup matching (by title, or by action/message+exact timestamp) is a best-effort heuristic, not a stable identity — a genuinely different row that happens to share the same title/action/timestamp would be skipped as a false "already exists" match.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched/created files. Confirmed `routeTree.gen.ts` picked up the renamed route. **Live-tested the real recovery path**: while verifying this feature, found the live database had actually been wiped (0 rows, all 20 tables) — ran the same restore logic for real and recovered 107 To-Dos, 54 Changelog entries, and 4 Audit Log entries from the existing CSV exports, plus re-ran `seed-workflows.ts`/`seed-models.ts` to restore those two registries; confirmed via direct row-count queries before and after. Added two Changelog entries (v2.14.0 for the feature, v2.15.0 for the incident recovery) and two To-Do items, both marked done.

### New page: CSV Import Wizard (`/import-wizard`); Data Manager: Reports section; Gallery: captions + drag-to-reorder

1. **Goal / behavior**: Three additions to exercise DB + Storage together. (a) New **Import Wizard** page — upload a `.csv`, preview its headers/sample rows, and import it either into Inventory (rows matched/updated by SKU, new SKUs inserted) or, when the columns don't match Inventory's shape, as a **generic import batch** whose rows are captured and browsable without any schema change. Past imports are listed with counts and a delete action. (b) Data Manager gained a **Reports** section (distinct from its existing raw-table-dump Exports) — "Analytics Summary" (30-day date/audits/errors/tasks-created CSV) and "Audit Summary" (audit events grouped by category/action/status with counts) are generated server-side and saved to `exports/`, listed separately from the raw exports with their own download/delete actions. (c) The Gallery page (theme image browser) can now caption any image (persisted, shown as an overlay on the thumbnail and editable from the preview dialog) and drag-and-drop thumbnails to reorder them (persisted); both gated on `databaseEnabled` on top of the page's existing `s3Enabled` gate.
2. **Data flow**: New tables `csv_import_batches` + `csv_import_rows` (batch metadata + per-row JSON for generic imports) and `gallery_image_meta` (`storage_key` PK, `caption`, `sort_order`) in `src/server/db/schema.ts`, migrated via `db:generate`/`db:migrate`. New `src/services/db/csvImportService.ts` (`previewCsvImport` downloads+parses via the existing `@/lib/csvImport` `parseCsv`, detects Inventory-shape via required-header check; `importCsv` upserts into `inventoryItems` by SKU or inserts a `csvImportRows` batch; list/getRows/delete). New `src/services/db/reportService.ts` — reuses the existing `rowsToCsv` (`@/lib/csvExport`) and the same `storage_files`-row-per-export pattern as `exportService.ts`, but computes formatted summaries (day-bucketed activity counts; audit group-by-counts) instead of raw table dumps; reports are tagged `["report", source]` and listed by filename prefix (`analytics-report-`/`audit-report-`) so they don't mix with the plain Exports list. New `src/services/db/galleryMetaService.ts` (list/upsert caption, bulk `reorderGalleryImages(orderedKeys)` that assigns `sortOrder = index`) layered on top of the existing read-only `themeGalleryService.ts` (S3 listing untouched). `gallery.tsx` merges `listThemeGalleryFiles()` with `listGalleryImageMeta()` client-side to sort/caption; drag state is plain component `useState` (`dragKey`/`dragOverKey`) using native HTML5 drag events, committed via `reorderMutation` on drop.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses `DATABASE_URL` and provisioned S3 storage. Import Wizard uploads through the existing `uploadStorageFile` (folder `uploads`) before parsing — no new upload path. True dynamic per-upload table creation was intentionally not attempted (this template's migration workflow — `db:generate`/`db:migrate`, no runtime DDL) — the generic `csv_import_rows` JSON-per-row table is the practical substitute for "a new table per new CSV shape."
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Inventory CSV detection is a fixed required-header check (`name`, `sku`, `category`, case-insensitive) — a CSV with differently-named columns for the same data would be captured generically instead of matched, with no column-remapping UI. Generic-import rows are opaque JSON blobs (browsable in a dialog, not queryable/filterable across batches). Reports read the full `audit_logs`/`todos`/`error_logs` tables (no pagination) — fine at this app's current data volumes, consistent with the rest of the app's read patterns. Gallery captions/order apply to the theme-asset image library specifically (per the user's explicit choice to extend that existing page rather than build a separate general photo gallery) — the drag reorder is a simple adjacent-swap-via-splice on the currently filtered/sorted list, not a full drag-preview/ghost-element implementation.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + one-time migration-tracking backfill (the same `drizzle.__drizzle_migrations` desync documented in earlier entries above — `audit_logs_id_seq already exists` on a fresh `db:migrate` even though tables 0000-0015 were already live; backfilled sha256 hashes for migrations 0000-0015 from `meta/_journal.json`, then re-ran `db:migrate` cleanly for migration 0016 only) + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `csv_import_batches`, `csv_import_rows`, `gallery_image_meta` in the table list). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only; fixed one real new error from an invalid dynamic-import namespace type reference). `bunx biome format --write` on all touched/created files. Confirmed `/import-wizard`, `/data-manager`, `/gallery` all return 200 from the dev server and `routeTree.gen.ts` picked up the new route.

### AI-powered features: Summariser citations, Data Explorer, Import Wizard AI tagging, Report narration, Insights Q&A, Workflow charts, Knowledge Base

1. **Goal / behavior**: Seven additions combining the database/storage work from the previous entry with AI/ML connectors, confirmed connector selection, and existing connector/workflow infrastructure. (a) **AI Summariser** now also returns quoted source citations under the summary (a "## Citations" section the prompt asks for, parsed client-side) and has an Anthropic/OpenAI provider picker (previously hardcoded to Anthropic). (b) New **Data Explorer** page (`/data-explorer`) — ask a question in plain English about this app's own database (Inventory, To-Dos, Audit/Error Log, Changelog, Storage, CSV import batches); an AI model turns it into a single SELECT query, a new server function validates it's read-only against an allow-list before running it, and results show in a table alongside the generated SQL. (c) **Import Wizard** gained an optional "Suggest missing categories with AI" step when importing into Inventory — fills blank `category` values before the import commits. (d) **Data Manager**'s Reports section gained a "Narrate with AI" action per saved report, producing a short plain-English paragraph stored on the existing `storage_files.extracted_text` column and shown inline. (e) **AI Insights** gained an "Ask a Question" tab next to the existing "Generate Insights" button — same live connector-table selection, but conversational, saved to the same `ai_insights` history (prefixed `**Q:** ... **A:** ...`). (f) **Workflows**' Run History tab gained two charts (runs/day, average duration/day, last 14 days) computed from the same `runs` query already loaded there. (g) New **Knowledge Base** page (`/knowledge-base`) — keyword-searches the AI Squared Vector Store connector's `document_vector_embeddings` table and lets you "Ask AI" a question answered from the top matches with `[doc N]` citations.
2. **Data flow**: New shared `src/lib/aiProviders.ts` (`AI_PROVIDERS` = Anthropic id 1319 / OpenAI id 1751, both confirmed with the user; `extractProviderText` handles both response shapes) and `src/components/ai/AiProviderSelect.tsx` (a `Select` wrapper), reused across all five AI-touching pages instead of each hardcoding a connector id or duplicating response-parsing. New `src/services/db/dataExplorerService.ts` — `EXPLORABLE_TABLES` (an explicit allow-list, deliberately excluding secrets-adjacent tables like `api_keys`/`webhooks`), `buildSchemaContext()` for the AI prompt, and `runNaturalLanguageQuery(sql)` which rejects anything but a single `SELECT` against an allow-listed table (regex-based keyword/table-reference checks — not a full SQL parser, since the input is AI-generated, not directly user-typed) before running it via `db.execute(sql.raw(...))` and coercing cell values to JSON-safe primitives (`createServerFn`'s serialization check rejected a raw `unknown`-typed result). `csvImportService.ts` gained `getAllCsvRows(key)` (full parse, not just the 5-row preview) and `importCsv` now accepts an optional `rows` override so AI-suggested categories merged client-side get imported directly instead of re-parsing the original CSV. `reportService`/`storageService` needed no changes — narration reuses the existing `getStorageFileText` + `updateStorageFileMeta({ extractedText })`. `insights.tsx`'s existing per-table `querySource` loading loop was extracted into `loadSelectedTableData()`/`summarizeTables()` so both "Generate Insights" and the new "Ask a Question" tab share it. `workflows.tsx`'s new `runTrend` memo buckets the already-loaded `runs` array into the last 14 calendar days (run count + average `durationMs`) — no new query. `knowledge-base.tsx` calls `useQuerySource()` directly against connector `1142` with an `ILIKE`-based query (own-generated string, single-quote-escaped) since no embeddings-generation endpoint is exposed through `useExecuteModel` for a true vector-similarity search.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Connector ids `1319` (Anthropic)/`1751` (OpenAI) and `1142` (AI Squared Vector Store) are the ones confirmed with the user via the mandatory connector-confirmation flow; `data-explorer.tsx` deliberately queries this app's own database directly rather than an external connector (also confirmed). AI provider choice is a per-page, component-local `useState` (not persisted) — every AI action defaults to Anthropic (`DEFAULT_AI_PROVIDER_ID`).
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Citations parsing on the Summariser is a plain heading-match (`## Citations`) + line-split — if the model doesn't follow the requested format exactly, citations silently come back empty rather than erroring (summary itself is unaffected either way). Data Explorer's SQL-safety gate is a deliberately conservative regex/table-allow-list check, not a real SQL parser — it blocks known-dangerous keywords and any table outside the allow-list, but a sufficiently adversarial query crafted to dodge the regex isn't provably impossible; acceptable here since the SQL is AI-generated from a fixed schema prompt, not directly user-supplied. Import Wizard's AI category suggestions require the AI's reply to have exactly one line per missing-category row in order — a mismatched count is rejected with a toast rather than guessed at. The Knowledge Base's search is keyword (`ILIKE`) against stored text/metadata, explicitly **not** true vector/cosine-similarity search — flagged to the user upfront in the plan and in the page's own UI copy — because generating a query embedding would need an embeddings API that isn't exposed through the available AI/ML connectors (only chat completions are). Workflow charts only reflect runs recorded through this app's own Run dialog (`workflow_runs` table), same limitation as the existing Run History tab; there's no token-usage field on that table so the second chart shows average duration instead of tokens.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors after fixing two real ones (an invalid `unknown`-typed server-fn return needing coercion to JSON-safe primitives in `dataExplorerService.ts`; a fragment-without-key issue in the Reports table needing `Fragment key=`) — same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only remains. `bunx biome format --write` on every touched/created file. Confirmed `/ai-summariser`, `/data-explorer`, `/knowledge-base`, `/import-wizard`, `/data-manager`, `/insights`, `/workflows` all return 200 from the dev server, and `routeTree.gen.ts` picked up the two new routes. Live-verified the vector store connector's schema (`document_vector_embeddings` columns) via `aisquared_connectors_discover` before building the Knowledge Base page, and confirmed both AI/ML connectors (1319/1751) have no custom `request_format` (so the `{ messages }` chat-provider path, not `{ payload }`, is correct) via `aisquared_connectors_show`.

### Knowledge Base: sample search keyword suggestions

1. **Goal / behavior**: The Knowledge Base page's search card now shows a row of clickable "sample keyword" chips (labelled "Try a sample keyword from your documents") above/below the search box; clicking one fills the search box with that term and runs the search immediately, giving users a starting point instead of guessing what to type into an empty search box.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. `knowledge-base.tsx` — on mount, a `useEffect` fires the existing `querySource` mutation against the same vector-store connector/table (`SELECT id, text, metadata, created_at ... LIMIT 50`) purely to derive suggestions (no new endpoint). A new pure helper `extractKeywordSuggestions(rows)` parses each row's `metadata` JSON for `filename` (stripped of its extension) and tokenizes `text` into words (>4 chars, lowercased, filtered against a small hardcoded stopword list), ranks words by frequency, and returns up to 8 unique suggestions (filenames first, then top words). `handleSearch` now accepts an optional `term` argument (defaults to the current search-box state) so a chip click can pass its own keyword straight through without needing to first update state and wait for a re-render.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses the confirmed vector-store connector (id `1142`) and table (`document_vector_embeddings`) already used by this page.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Keyword extraction is a simple frequency/stopword heuristic over a 50-row sample, not a real keyword-extraction/NLP model (no such API is exposed by the available connectors) — with very little data in the knowledge base (currently a single test document), suggestions may be sparse or generic; they improve automatically as more documents are added. The suggestion chip row is hidden entirely if the sample fetch fails or returns no usable words/filenames, rather than showing an empty/error state, since it's a supplementary convenience rather than a required feature.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors in the touched file (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on the touched file. Live-queried the vector store connector via `aisquared_connectors_query_source` to confirm the current sample data shape before implementing. Added a Changelog entry (v2.16.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done.

### Knowledge Base: search across all vector store tables

1. **Goal / behavior**: The Knowledge Base page now searches all three document/embedding tables in the AI Squared Vector Store connector (`document_vector_embeddings`, `lightning_embedding`, `seemore_embedding`) instead of just one, surfacing documents that were previously invisible to search. A new "Table" dropdown next to the search box lets the user narrow to "All tables" (default) or a single one. Each result card and the AI-answer citations now show which table a snippet came from.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. `knowledge-base.tsx` — `VECTOR_TABLE` (singular) became `VECTOR_TABLES` (a fixed 3-item tuple) plus a `VectorTable` union type; a new `buildUnionQuery(tables, where, limit)` helper generates a `SELECT ... UNION ALL SELECT ...` query (one branch per table, each tagging its rows with a `source_table` literal column) so results from every table are ranked together by `created_at` in one round trip through the existing `useQuerySource()` hook — no new server code. `VectorRow` gained `source_table`; the on-mount keyword-suggestion sampler and `handleSearch` both use `buildUnionQuery` (the latter respecting the new table-filter `Select`, defaulting to all three tables). `handleAsk`'s AI prompt context now includes `(table: ..., id: ...)` per snippet.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; still the same confirmed connector (id `1142`). The connector's fourth table, `fortune1000` (structured company/financial data — rank, sector, revenue, etc.), is deliberately excluded — confirmed with the user via the `question` tool, since it has a completely different shape and isn't a document table.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Still keyword (`ILIKE`) search, not vector/embedding similarity search, same limitation as before — this change only widens which tables are searched, not the search method. If `fortune1000` (or other structured connector data) needs to be searchable later, it would need its own query path/UI treatment rather than joining the union, since its columns don't match the document shape.
5. **Validation**: Live-verified the connector's full catalog via `aisquared_connectors_discover` (found 4 streams total) and ran the actual 3-table `UNION ALL` query live via `aisquared_connectors_query_source` before wiring it into the app, confirming real rows come back tagged with the correct `source_table` from all three tables. `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors in the touched file (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on the touched file. Added a Changelog entry (v2.17.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done.

### Fix: AI features moved off the failing AI/ML connector to direct OPENAI_API_KEY/ANTHROPIC_API_KEY secrets; Knowledge Base combines chunked results per document

1. **Goal / behavior**: (a) Every Anthropic/OpenAI-powered feature in the app (AI Summariser, Data Explorer, Data Manager report narration, Import Wizard AI category suggestions, AI Insights' "Ask a Question", Knowledge Base's "Ask AI", and two of AI Chat's three model options) now calls the OpenAI/Anthropic REST APIs directly using this app's own `OPENAI_API_KEY`/`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` secrets, instead of going through the platform's AI/ML connector (ids 1319/1751), whose auth had started failing with 401s on every call. This directly fixes the two entries that were sitting unresolved in the Error Log. (b) Knowledge Base search results are no longer split one-card-per-chunk — chunks belonging to the same source document are now combined into a single result entry, in original order, so a document that matched in 3 chunks shows as 1 result (tagged "3 chunks combined") instead of 3 disconnected fragments.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. New `src/services/ai/aiModelService.ts` — `callAiModel` (`createServerFn` + `requireAppAccess()`) reads `process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY`/`process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` directly (not through the base `env.ts`, which doesn't define these) and calls `https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages` or `https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions` with `fetch`, returning a unified `{ data: [{ choices: [{ message: { content } }] }] }` shape that matches what the old connector-based `executeModel` returned — this let every page keep its existing `extractProviderText(res?.data)` parsing unchanged. New `src/hooks/useAiModel.ts` mirrors `useExecuteModel()`'s `.mutate({ connectorId, messages, instructions })` call shape (just using `"anthropic"|"openai"` instead of a numeric connector id), so call sites in `ai-summariser.tsx`, `data-explorer.tsx`, `data-manager.tsx`, `import-wizard.tsx`, and `knowledge-base.tsx` only needed their hook import/instantiation swapped — no other logic changed. `src/lib/aiProviders.ts`'s `AI_PROVIDERS` ids changed from `1319`/`1751` (connector ids) to `"anthropic"`/`"openai"` (provider keys); `AiProviderSelect.tsx` updated to pass the string value through instead of `Number()`-coercing it. `insights.tsx` now holds both `executeModel` (still used for the fixed "AI Squared Bolt 2" connector, id 1803, in "Generate Insights") and the new `aiModel` (used for the provider-picker-driven "Ask a Question"). `chat.tsx` — added `providerForConnectorId(id)` so `handleSend` routes connector ids 1319/1751 through `useAiModel()` and only 1803 through the old `useExecuteModel()`; `extractReply` was simplified to a single OpenAI-shaped parse path since Anthropic replies now also arrive pre-normalized to that shape from `aiModelService`. Knowledge Base — new `parseMetadata()`/`groupResultsByDocument()` helpers in `knowledge-base.tsx` merge chunk rows sharing the same `source_table` + `knowledge_base_file_id`/filename into one `DocumentGroup` (sorted by numeric/string id before joining text), computed via a `useMemo` off the raw `results`; the results list, its count, and the "Ask AI" context-building now all operate on these grouped documents instead of raw chunk rows.
3. **Inputs/config**: `OPENAI_API_KEY` is already present in this app's `.env.local`/environment (pre-existing secret, not added by this change). **`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is not currently set** in this environment — Anthropic-routed calls will fail with a clear "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is not configured" error (thrown before any fetch) until that secret is added; OpenAI-routed calls (and the default provider can be switched to OpenAI in the picker) work today. No new schema/DB config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: The "AI Squared Bolt 2" model option (in AI Chat and Insights' "Generate Insights") intentionally still goes through the platform connector (id 1803) — it isn't one of the two secret-backed providers, so it wasn't touched, and would still fail if the connector itself has an auth problem (that wasn't part of this fix's scope, confirmed with the user). Document grouping in Knowledge Base is based on `metadata.knowledge_base_file_id` (falling back to `filename`, then the row's own `id`) — a document uploaded without that metadata field would show as one group per chunk again, same as before. `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` still needs to be added as a secret for the Anthropic option to actually work end-to-end.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors across all nine touched/created files (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on every touched/created file. Confirmed via a direct read-only DB query that the Error Log had exactly 2 unresolved 401 entries before the fix (both from the AIS connector), then marked both `resolved: true` after implementing the fix. Added two Changelog entries (v2.18.0 fix, v2.19.0 improvement) and three To-Do items, all marked done.

### Fix: Knowledge Base combined results looked choppy/repetitive

1. **Goal / behavior**: Combined per-document results on the Knowledge Base page no longer repeat text at chunk boundaries or look visually cramped. Each result now reads as one continuous passage, with a cleaner header (file icon + filename, grouped badges) and long documents collapse to a short readable preview with a "Show full document"/"Show less" toggle instead of a small fixed-height scrollbox.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. `knowledge-base.tsx` — new `stitchChunkText(a, b)` finds the longest matching suffix-of-`a`/prefix-of-`b` (checked from a 400-char cap down to 20 chars) and drops the duplicate before concatenating; `groupResultsByDocument` now builds each document's combined `text` via `sortedRows.map(r => r.text).reduce(stitchChunkText)` instead of a plain `.join("\n\n")`. New `expandedDocs` (a `Set<string>` of document keys) state, reset on every new search, drives a per-document expand/collapse toggle; a `PREVIEW_CHAR_LIMIT` (600 chars) constant decides whether a document is truncated (with a trailing "…") until expanded. The result card markup was restructured: a header row (file icon + truncated filename, then a badge group for source table + "N chunks merged"), a `leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line` text block (previously `whitespace-pre-wrap` inside a `max-h-64 overflow-y-auto` box, which visually clipped mid-paragraph), and a ghost `Button` (`ChevronDown`/`ChevronUp` icon) shown only when the document exceeds the preview limit.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Overlap detection is an exact-substring match (not fuzzy/whitespace-tolerant) — verified live against real sample chunks from this app's own knowledge base (correctly removed a ~250-character duplicated overlap, matching the chunking config the seeded test document itself documents), but a chunk pipeline with different overlap behavior or lightly-edited overlapping text (e.g. re-tokenized whitespace) wouldn't be caught and would fall back to a plain paragraph-break join, same as before this fix. The numbered "doc 1/2/3" badge was removed from each card (filename + table badge now identify a result instead) — acceptable since filenames are usually present in the sample data's metadata; a result with neither a filename nor `knowledge_base_file_id` still falls back to using its own row id as the group key, so it won't merge with anything, same as before.
5. **Validation**: Verified `stitchChunkText` against two real, adjacent chunks queried live from the connector via `aisquared_connectors_query_source` (documented in the vector-store table entries above) — confirmed it removed the duplicated overlap and produced a shorter, non-repeating combined string. `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write` on the touched file. Added a Changelog entry (v2.20.0) and a To-Do item documenting the fix, marked done.

### Polish: Knowledge Base results visually beautified

1. **Goal / behavior**: Each Knowledge Base result card now has a small file-icon chip, a friendly source-table label (instead of the raw table name like `document_vector_embeddings`), a "chunks merged" badge with a `Layers` icon, a divider between the header and the passage, a subtle hover highlight, and — most usefully — the current search term is highlighted (`<mark>`) everywhere it appears inside the combined document text, so a user can immediately see why a result matched instead of re-reading the whole passage.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/data change — presentation only. `knowledge-base.tsx` — new `TABLE_LABELS` (a `Record<VectorTable, string>` map to human-readable names) and `highlightMatches(text, term)` (splits the shown text on a case-insensitive regex of the trimmed search term, escaped via a small `escapeRegExp` helper, and wraps matches in `<mark className="bg-primary/20 ...">`), applied to `shownText` (the same preview/expanded text used by the earlier "combine chunks" work) right before rendering. The result card markup gained an icon chip (`bg-muted` rounded square + `FileText`), a `Separator` between the header row and the text, and `hover:border-primary/40 hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors` on the card itself.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Highlighting is a literal case-insensitive substring match against the exact search box term (not stemmed/fuzzy) — clicking a suggestion chip highlights that chip's word, but a multi-word query only highlights exact occurrences of the full phrase, consistent with how the underlying `ILIKE` search itself matches. `TABLE_LABELS` is a fixed map for the 3 known document tables — a 4th table would need a manual label added (falls back to showing nothing worse than the existing `sourceTable` value if not, since the map covers exactly `VectorTable`'s type today).
