### User Roles & Permissions: matrix view, search, bulk actions, duplicate role

1. **Goal / behavior**: Replaced the previous layout (one full page-list table per role, repeated per role card) with a single compact matrix: pages grouped by their real nav section (as sub-header rows) down the left, one column per role, checkboxes at each intersection. Added a search box (filters rows by page title or section label), per-role "All"/"None" quick-grant buttons in each column header, a page-count badge per role, and a Duplicate action (icon button in the column header) that clones a role's name (suffixed "(copy)"), color, description, and full permission set as a new role.
2. **Data flow**: No new tables. `roleService.ts` gained `duplicateRole(roleId)` — reads the source role + its `role_permissions` rows, inserts a new `roles` row, then bulk-inserts matching `role_permissions` rows for the copy. `roles-permissions.tsx` — `groupNavEntries(navRoutes)` turns the sidebar's own `routes` config into `{label, items}[]` sections (standalone top-level items like Dashboard/Theme/Settings are bucketed into a synthetic "General" section) instead of using the previously-flattened `flattenNavEntries` list; a `filteredSections` memo applies the search term to both page titles and section labels. `grantAll(role)`/`revokeAll(role)` both call the existing `setRolePermissions` (full-replace) with either every page title or an empty array.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. Search/filter state is component-local (resets on reload), matching this app's convention for other local-only list-view filters.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Same as before — this remains a reference/documentation matrix, not enforced access control (sign-in is platform-managed). The matrix's first column is `sticky left-0` for horizontal scroll on narrow screens with many roles, but there's no virtualization — a very large number of roles (dozens) would make the table wide and less scannable; acceptable at this app's expected scale (a handful of roles).
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors across both touched files (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` on both touched files — no new lint issues (fixed one real `useConst` suggestion during the edit). Added a Changelog entry (v3.2.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done, and logged this turn to the Chat Log.

### Roles & Permissions: added a Team Members directory (reference-only, no login)

1. **Goal / behavior**: User asked to let users assign Roles/Permissions to other users. Since this app's sign-in is fully platform-managed (no custom users table, no passwords per project rules), this was scoped down and confirmed with the user as a **Team Members directory** instead — a second tab ("Team Members") on `/roles-permissions` where a team member (name, email, optional title) can be added/edited/removed and assigned one existing Role from the Permissions Matrix tab. Purely a reference list, same non-enforcement caveat as the Roles matrix itself.
2. **Data flow**: New `team_members` table in `src/server/db/schema.ts` (`name`, `email`, `title` nullable, `roleId` nullable FK → `roles.id` with `onDelete: "set null"`), migrated via `db:generate`/`db:migrate` (migration `0038_motionless_silverclaw.sql`). New `src/services/db/teamMemberService.ts` (`listTeamMembers` left-joins `roles` for display name/color, `createTeamMember`/`updateTeamMember`/`deleteTeamMember`, standard `createServerFn` + `requireAppAccess()` pattern). `roles-permissions.tsx` — wrapped the existing matrix content in a `Tabs` ("Permissions Matrix" / "Team Members"); the new `TeamMembersPanel` component is a standard `useQuery`/`useMutation` CRUD table (name/email/title/role columns), using the app's established conditional-mounting dialog pattern (`{dialogOpen && <Dialog>...}`).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses `DATABASE_URL`. A member with no role assigned shows "No role" instead of a blank cell.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: This does **not** create real user accounts, login credentials, or enforce access in any way — identical caveat to the Roles matrix. Deleting a Role sets any assigned members' `roleId` to `null` (they become "No role") rather than blocking the delete or cascading.
5. **Validation**: `bun run db:generate` + `bun run db:migrate` + `bun run db:status` (confirmed `team_members` in the table list). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors across both created/touched files (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` on both files — no new lint issues. Added a Changelog entry (v3.3.0) and a To-Do item documenting the feature, marked done, and logged this turn to the Chat Log.

### Team Members: clarified as invited/access-user list, disabled when app is Public

1. **Goal / behavior**: Two follow-ups to the Team Members directory above. (a) Its description now explicitly frames it as a manually-maintained list of people invited to or with access to this app (there is no platform API this app can call to read the live invite list automatically — confirmed by searching `/base/src/server` for any users/invite-listing endpoint; only `assertAppAccess`/`getAuthState` exist, which check a *single* token's access, not enumerate all invited users). (b) The Team Members tab is now disabled (with an explanatory `Alert` in its place) whenever this app is published as Public, since a public app (`AIS_AUTH_ENABLED=false`, no sign-in at all) has no concept of "who has access" to track.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/service change. `roles-permissions.tsx` — added a `useQuery(["authState"], () => getAuthState())` (the same `@/server/auth` `createServerFn` already called this way by `app-layout.tsx`, confirmed as a valid client-side pattern before reusing it here); `isPublicApp = authState?.authEnabled === false` disables the "Team Members" `TabsTrigger` and, defensively, a `useEffect` snaps `activeTab` back to `"matrix"` if it was somehow left on `"members"` when the app is/becomes public. The `TabsContent` for `"members"` renders the explanatory `Alert` instead of `TeamMembersPanel` when `isPublicApp` is true. `TeamMembersPanel`'s intro copy was reworded to describe the manual-upkeep expectation directly.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config; reuses the platform's existing `AIS_AUTH_ENABLED` server env (surfaced to the client only through `getAuthState()`'s `authEnabled` field, never the raw env var itself).
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: This still cannot auto-populate real invited users — "invited/has access" is aspirational copy describing what the admin should manually keep the list in sync with, not a live sync. If the platform ever exposes a users/invite-listing endpoint through this app's own tRPC/service layer, this page could be revisited to auto-populate from it instead of manual entry.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors in the touched file (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` on the touched file — no new lint issues. Added a Changelog entry (v3.4.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done, and logged this turn to the Chat Log.

### Team Members: "Add Myself" shortcut (list starts empty by design)

1. **Goal / behavior**: User expected to see themselves already listed in Team Members. Since this list has no platform API to auto-populate real invited users (documented in the entry above), it always starts empty — which reads as a bug on first use. Added an "Add Myself" button (next to "Add Member", both gated on `dbEnabled`) that prefills the Add Member dialog with the real signed-in user's name/email; it's hidden once that email already appears in the list.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/service change. `TeamMembersPanel` — added `useGetUser({ enabled: dbEnabled })` (the existing base `@/hooks/useAuth` hook, tRPC `auth.me`) to read the real current user's `attributes.name`/`attributes.email`; a new `isSelfListed` memo-less check (`.some()` over the already-fetched `members` list, case-insensitive email match) decides whether to show the button; `openCreateSelf()` calls the existing `resetMemberForm()` then sets `name`/`email` from `currentUser` before opening the same dialog `openCreate()` uses — no new dialog/component.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. On a public app (`AIS_AUTH_ENABLED=false`), `auth.me` returns `{ data: null }` by design (see `/base/src/server/trpc/routers/auth.ts`), so `currentUser` is `null` and the button simply doesn't render — consistent with Team Members already being disabled entirely for public apps per the entry above.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: This only prefills name/email — it still requires a manual click and (optionally) picking a Role/Title before saving; it does not silently auto-insert the current user as a row. If the signed-in user's email is later changed  through the platform, this page won't detect the mismatch retroactively (the `isSelfListed` check just runs on load).
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors in the touched file (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` on the touched file — no new lint issues. Added a Changelog entry (v3.5.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done, and logged this turn to the Chat Log.

### Team Members: current user added automatically (replaces the manual "Add Myself" button)

1. **Goal / behavior**: Per a direct follow-up ("can't you do it automatically?"), the "Add Myself" button from the entry above was removed — the signed-in user is now inserted into Team Members on their own, automatically, the first time they open the page, using their real name/email. "Add Member" remains for adding everyone else.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. `TeamMembersPanel` — a new `autoAddSelfMutation` (same `createTeamMember` call the manual button used) plus a `useRef<boolean>` guard (`autoAddAttempted`) and a `useEffect` that fires once `dbEnabled`, `currentUser.attributes.email`, and `members` have all loaded: if the current user's email isn't already in the list and this effect hasn't already attempted an insert, it calls `autoAddSelfMutation.mutate(...)` with `name: currentUser.attributes.name || currentUser.attributes.email` (falls back to the email itself if the platform user has no display name) and invalidates `["teamMembers"]` on success so the new row appears immediately. The ref guard (not just checking `isSelfListed`) exists specifically so a slow insert can't be double-fired by a re-render before the list has refetched and reflected it.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. On a public app, `currentUser` is `null` (per the entry above), so the effect's guard clause simply skips — consistent with Team Members being disabled entirely for public apps.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: This only runs once per mount (guarded by the ref) — if the automatic insert ever fails (e.g. a transient network error), the user won't be silently retried on every render, but also won't be retried at all without a full page reload (the ref resets on remount). There is intentionally no visible feedback (toast/spinner) for this automatic insert, to keep it unobtrusive; the user simply sees themselves appear in the table.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors in the touched file (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` on the touched file — no new lint issues. Added a Changelog entry (v3.6.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done, and logged this turn to the Chat Log.

### Team Members: auto-registration moved from the Roles & Permissions page to the shared app layout

1. **Goal / behavior**: The previous entry's auto-add-on-mount logic only ran while a user had the Roles & Permissions page's Team Members tab open, so anyone who never visited that specific page (or lacked a reason to) would never get auto-added — a real gap, since the whole point was "everyone with access to this app should end up listed." The signed-in user is now registered the moment they load **any** page in the app.
2. **Data flow**: No schema change. New `src/hooks/useAutoRegisterTeamMember.ts` — a self-contained hook (`useCapabilities()` for `databaseEnabled`, a `useQuery(["teamMembers"], listTeamMembers)`, a `useMutation(createTeamMember)`, and a `useRef` + `useEffect` guard identical in spirit to the removed per-page version) that takes `{ authEnabled, userName, userEmail }` as params and inserts the user into `team_members` once if their email isn't already listed. Mounted from `src/components/layout/app-layout.tsx` (`AppLayout`, which wraps every page via `_protected.tsx` — confirmed this app is fully multi-page, so there's no coverage gap), passed the same `authState.authEnabled`/`user.data.attributes.name`/`.email` values `AppLayout` already computes for its own header/sign-out UI — no duplicate `getAuthState()`/`useGetUser()` calls were added. `TeamMembersPanel` (`roles-permissions.tsx`) had its now-redundant per-page copy of this exact logic (`useGetUser`, `isSelfListed`, the mutation, the `useRef`/`useEffect`) removed entirely — the panel now only lists/edits/deletes, relying on the global hook to have already added the current user by the time they get here.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. On a public app (`authEnabled === false`), `AppLayout` never fetches `useGetUser()` in the first place (existing behavior, unchanged), so `userEmail` is `undefined` and the hook's `ready` guard skips entirely — consistent with Team Members being disabled app-wide for public apps (documented in an earlier entry above).
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: Still only fires once per hook-mount (guarded by a `useRef`), not persistently retried — same tradeoff as the removed per-page version, just now evaluated on every page's first load instead of one specific page's. This does not change anything about *who* can be auto-added (still just the currently signed-in user, once) — it only changes *where* that registration is triggered from.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors across all three touched/created files (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only). `bunx biome format --write`/`check` on all three files — no new lint issues. Added a Changelog entry (v3.7.0) and a To-Do item documenting the change, marked done, and logged this turn to the Chat Log.

### Trimmed the app: merged 7 monitoring/dev-tool pages into one "System Monitor" page

1. **Goal / behavior**: Per an explicit "trim down the whole app" request, consolidated 7 separate monitoring/dev-tool pages — System Status, Integration Health, Performance, Audit Log, Error Log, Query Console, Chat Log — into one new page, `/system` ("System Monitor"), with a tab per former page. Nothing about each tool's own functionality changed; only their navigation footprint did (7 nav entries and 7 routes became 1 of each). Kept the Changelog and To-Do pages exactly as-is per the user's explicit choice (they also back this project's logging conventions). Only removed/merged pages — no non-trivial code-cleanup pass was done in the same turn (scoped to the page consolidation only, per the user's answers).
2. **Data flow**: No schema/service change — every underlying service call (`system-status`'s health checks, `audit`'s `listAuditLogsPaged`, `error-log`'s CRUD, `integration-health`'s connector tests, `query`'s `useQuerySource`, `chat-log`'s CRUD, `performance`'s live sampling) is untouched. Each former route's component function was moved as-is into `src/components/system/<Name>Panel.tsx` (same function body, just exported instead of being a route's `component`), and `src/routes/_protected/system.tsx` renders all 7 inside a `Tabs`/`TabsContent` (Base UI `Tabs`, which only mounts the active panel's DOM — confirmed via `/base/src/components/ui/tabs.tsx`'s `TabsPrimitive.Panel`), so Performance's live-sampling `requestAnimationFrame`/`setInterval` loops still only run while that specific tab is open, same as when it was its own page.
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config. `src/components/layout/index.ts` — removed the 6 now-merged nav entries from Platform/Monitoring & Logs/Configuration and added one "System Monitor" entry (icon `ServerCog`) to Monitoring & Logs. The Dashboard's `quickLinks` (`index.tsx`), `GlobalSearch.tsx`'s keyword map, `globalSearchService.ts`'s error-result link, and `savedQueryService.ts`'s audit-log `page` field were all repointed from the old individual URLs (`/audit`, `/error-log`, `/performance`, `/integration-health`, `/query`, `/chat-log`, `/system-status`) to `/system`.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: There's no deep-linking to a specific tab yet (e.g. `/system?tab=audit`) — visiting `/system` always opens on the Status tab; a user following an old bookmark to e.g. `/audit` will get a 404 rather than redirecting into the merged page's Audit tab, since no redirect routes were added (out of scope for a page-consolidation pass). Each panel's own internal behavior (pagination, filters, live sampling, alert thresholds, etc.) is completely unchanged — only reachable via a tab click instead of a sidebar link now.
5. **Validation**: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p .` — no new errors across all created/touched files (same pre-existing unrelated `ve.vite.config.ts` error only; fixed two real new errors — an unused `History` icon import and a missing `ServerCog` import in `index.tsx`). `bunx biome format --write` on all created/touched files; `bunx biome check` showed only the same pre-existing `useExhaustiveDependencies`/`noArrayIndexKey` patterns already present in the moved files before this change (documented in earlier entries above) — not new regressions. Confirmed `routeTree.gen.ts` picks up `/system` and no longer lists the 6 removed routes.

### Removed Query Console tab from System Monitor

1. **Goal / behavior**: Per an explicit request, removed the "Query Console" tab from the consolidated `/system` ("System Monitor") page — the page now has 6 tabs (Status, Integration Health, Performance, Audit Log, Error Log, Chat Log) instead of 7.
2. **Data flow**: No schema/service change. `system.tsx` — removed the `QueryPage` import and its entry from the `TABS` config array (the tab list/content are both driven by that array, so no other JSX change was needed). Deleted the now-unused `src/components/system/QueryPanel.tsx` outright (confirmed via a repo-wide search it was referenced nowhere else).
3. **Inputs/config**: No new env/config.
4. **Constraints / known limitations**: There is no longer any in-app page for running ad-hoc SQL against a source connector — that capability is fully removed, not relocated elsewhere.
5. **Validation**: `bunx biome format --write` on the touched route file. Confirmed no remaining references to the removed panel via a repo-wide search.
